Extensive experiments on six standard ABSA benchmarks show that GHI outperforms all baselines on the SemEval domains, and multi-seed evaluations show stable improvements over strong DeBERTa. Further experiments show that with only 247M parameters, GHI approaches the performance of 11B Flan-T5 based methods on the ISE benchmark. Moreover, it demonstrates strong robustness on the challenging ARTS datasets, maintaining highly competitive performance where traditional models degrade. These results demonstrate that compact structural reasoning remains a valuable alternative to scale-driven approaches for fine-grained tasks.

</details>


### [35] [IdioLink: Retrieving Meaning Beyond Words Across Idiomatic and Literal Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22247)
*Kai Golan Hashiloni,Daniel Fadlon,Lior Livyatan,Ofri Hefetz,Jiahuan Pei,Kfir Bar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IdioLink基準，用於測試模型將慣用語表達與字面或解釋性等價意義進行關聯的能力。基準包含10,700文檔、2,140查詢，涵蓋107個慣用語。研究發現現有嵌入模型在跨表面形式差異理解慣用語語義方面存在明顯不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慣用語理解是語言模型面臨的根本挑戰，因為慣用語的意義無法從表面形式推斷，需要超越詞彙重疊的語義抽象能力。

Method: 構建了一個包含10,700份文檔和2,140條查詢的大規模檢索基準，涵蓋107個慣用語的literal和figurative兩種用法，並對每個文檔和查詢中傳達核心語義的span進行了標註。

Result: 評估發現當前模型（BGE, E5, Contriever, Qwen）在跨表面形式差異檢索語義等價內容時表現掙扎，依賴於主題和淺層語義線索而非真正的慣用語理解。

Conclusion: IdioLink提供了一個具有挑戰性的測試平台，能夠暴露當前模型在慣用語感知語義檢索方面的關鍵差距，為未來模型改進提供方向。

Abstract: Idioms pose a fundamental challenge for language models, as their meaning cannot be inferred from surface form alone. Understanding such expressions, therefore, requires semantic abstraction beyond lexical overlap. We introduce IdioLink, a retrieval benchmark designed to test whether models can link idiomatic expressions to conceptually equivalent meanings expressed in literal or paraphrased forms. IdioLink comprises 10,700 documents and 2,140 queries, spanning 107 idioms with both literal and figurative uses. Each document and query is annotated with spans that convey the core meaning. Evaluating strong embedding baselines (e.g., BGE, E5, Contriever, and Qwen), we show that current models struggle to retrieve equivalent meanings across divergent surface realizations, relying instead on topical and shallow semantic cues. IdioLink exposes key gaps in idiom-aware semantic retrieval and provides a challenging testbed for future models.

</details>


### [36] [Harder to Defend: Towards Chinese Toxicity Attacks via Implicit Enhancement and Obfuscation Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22258)
*Jingyi Kang,Junyu Lu,Bo Xu,Hongbo Wang,Linlin zong,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CITA是一个受控的红队评估与防御数据生成框架，通过三阶段生成中文隐式毒性攻击样本，揭示现有检测器的漏洞，并证明生成的对抗数据可有效提升防御模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM毒性评估主要关注显式表达，忽视了隐式毒性的检测挑战；中文场景中，毒性常结合语义间接性与表面混淆（如拼音、谐音），相关研究不足。

Method: CITA采用三阶段流水线：有害意图学习、隐式毒性增强、混淆变体重写；CITD通过CITA生成的红色团队数据进行微调训练。

Result: 在CITA生成的评估样本上，7个检测器平均攻击成功率(ASR)达69.48%，存在显著的漏检风险；人类评估确认了毒性的有害性、隐含性与规避性均得到保留。

Conclusion: CITA框架可用于生成防御训练数据，通过CITD模型验证了其在提升检测器鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) require robust toxicity evaluation beyond explicit wording. This setting remains underexplored in Chinese, where toxicity may combine semantic indirectness with surface obfuscation. We introduce Chinese Implicit Toxicity Attack (CITA), a controlled red-team evaluation and defense-data generation framework, not a deployable evasion tool. CITA uses three stages: (i) Harmful Intent Learning, (ii) Implicit Toxicity Enhancement, and (iii) Obfuscation Variant Rewriting, to preserve harmful intent, increase implicitness, and add controlled surface variants. On CITA-generated evaluation samples, the seven tested detectors exhibit substantial missed-detection risks, reaching an average ASR of 69.48%; human evaluation further confirms preserved harmfulness and increased implicitness/evasiveness. As a downstream defense application, we fine-tune a Chinese Implicit Toxicity Defense model (CITD) with CITA-generated red-team data, showing that such data can improve robustness through additional training.

</details>


### [37] [Pattern-and-root inflectional morphology: the Arabic broken plural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22310)
*Alexis Amid Neme,Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper presents a pattern-and-root Arabic morphology model that inverts the traditional Semitic approach by prioritizing patterns over roots. It provides a detailed taxonomy for noun inflection, particularly for broken plurals (classifying triliteral BPs into 22 patterns/90 classes and quadriliteral BPs into 3 patterns/70 classes), separates inflection from derivation/semantics, and demonstrates practical application through 3,200 dictionary entries.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional Arabic morphology models rely on root-and-pattern as primary, which complicates practical linguistic resource management. There is a need to improve dictionary usability, handle broken plurals systematically, and create a simpler, more orderly taxonomy for noun inflection that separates inflection from derivation and semantics.

Method: The approach reverses the traditional Semitic root-and-pattern model to pattern-and-root, treating patterns as primary over roots. Inflectional description is separated from derivation and semantics. Vowel quantity is simplified to v/vv notation, while root alternations and orthographic variations are encoded factually. The taxonomy classifies triliteral BP nouns into 22 patterns subdivided into 90 classes, and quadriliteral BP nouns into 3 patterns subdivided into 70 classes, with inflectional variations expanding to 300 total inflectional classes.

Result: The model successfully applies to 3,200 broken plural noun entries with a straightforward encoding scheme. The taxonomy achieves sufficient detail with 160 base classes expanding to 300 inflectional classes when considering singular variations.

Conclusion: The model provides a practical taxonomy for Arabic noun inflection with 160 base classes expanding to 300 inflectional classes, successfully applied to 3,200 BP noun entries, enabling direct morphological analysis via dictionary lookup without morphophonological rules.

Abstract: We present a substantially implemented model of description of the inflectional morphology of Arabic nouns, with special attention to the management of dictionaries and other language resources by Arabic-speaking linguists. The breakthrough lies in the reversal of the traditional root-and-pattern Semitic model into pattern-and-root, giving precedence to patterns over roots. Our model includes broken plurals (BPs), i.e. plurals formed by modifying the stem. It is based on the traditional notions of root and pattern of Semitic morphology. However, as compared to traditional Arabic morphology, it keeps the formal description of inflection separate from that of derivation and semantics. As traditional Arabic dictionaries, the updatable dictionary is structured in lexical entries for lemmas, and the reference spelling is fully diacritized. In our model, morphological analysis of Arabic text is performed directly with a dictionary of words and without morphophonological rules. Our taxonomy for noun inflection is simple, orderly and detailed. We simplify the taxonomy of singular patterns by specifying vowel quantity as v or vv, and ignoring vowel quality. Root alternations and orthographical variations are encoded independently from patterns and in a factual way, without deep roots or morphophonological or orthographical rules. Nouns with a triliteral BP are classified according to 22 patterns subdivided into 90 classes, and nouns with a quadriliteral BP according to 3 patterns subdivided into 70 classes. These 160 classes become 300 inflectional classes when we take into account inflectional variations that affect only the singular. We provide a straightforward encoding scheme that we applied to 3 200 entries of BP nouns.

</details>


### [38] [TransitLM: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Map-Free Transit Route Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22355)
*Hanyu Guo,Jiedong Yang,Chao Chen,Longfei Xu,Kaikui Liu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TransitLM是一个大规模公共交通路线规划数据集，包含来自中国4个城市的超过1300万条路线规划记录，涵盖120,845个站点和13,666条线路，旨在使LLM能够在不依赖地图基础设施的情况下学习端到端的路线规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公共交通路线规划依赖于结构化地图基础设施和复杂的路径引擎，缺乏可用于训练模型绕过这一依赖性的现有数据集。

Method: 构建了包含预训练语料和基准测试数据的大规模数据集，设计了3个评估任务及相应指标，在数据集上训练LLM模型进行路线生成和GPS坐标到站点的隐式映射。

Result: 训练后的LLM能够高精度生成结构有效的路线，并在没有任何显式映射的情况下隐式地将任意GPS坐标匹配到适当站点。

Conclusion: 公共交通路线规划可完全从数据中学习，实现直接从起终点信息出发的端到端、无地图路线生成。

Abstract: Public transit route planning traditionally depends on structured map infrastructure and complex routing engines, and no existing dataset supports training models to bypass this dependency. We present TransitLM, a large-scale dataset of over 13 million transit route planning records from four Chinese cities covering 120,845 stations and 13,666 lines, released as a continual pre-training corpus and benchmark data for three evaluation tasks with complementary metrics. Experiments show that an LLM trained on TransitLM produces structurally valid routes at high accuracy and implicitly grounds arbitrary GPS coordinates to appropriate stations without any explicit mapping. These results demonstrate that transit route planning can be learned entirely from data, enabling end-to-end, map-free route generation directly from origin-destination information. The dataset and benchmark are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/GD-ML/TransitLM, with evaluation code at https://github.com/HotTricker/TransitLM.

</details>


### [39] [Modeling Pathology-Like Behavioral Patterns in Language Models Through Behavioral Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22356)
*Nicola Milano,Davide Marocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过在合成数据集上微调transformer语言模型，诱导抑郁、偏执等适应性不良行为模式，发现模型产生稳定的跨情境行为偏差和概率分布变化，验证了LLMs可作为研究行为-语言生成关系的受控认知计算平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLMs作为计算工具用于建模类人行为的探索，旨在通过结构化决策任务修改模型策略。

Method: 行为归纳框架：通过在受适应性不良行为模式启发的合成数据集上微调transformer语言模型，使其在多样情境中选择特定类别的动作。

Result: 微调模型产生稳定的、跨情境的下一token概率分布偏移，增加负性和威胁相关解读的概率；不同行为模式优化产生可区分的反应倾向，表明行为训练产生差异化的策略级偏差而非通用分布偏斜。

Conclusion: LLMs可作为基于策略的系统，其中行为约束塑造涌现的表征结构，可作为研究行为、解释与生成语言之间关系的受控实验平台。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as computational tools for modeling human-like behavior. We introduce a behavioral induction framework that modifies model policies through fine-tuning on structured decision-making tasks: using synthetic datasets inspired by maladaptive behavioral patterns, including depression and paranoia, we train transformer-based language models to consistently select specific classes of actions across diverse contexts. We then test whether this behavioral optimization produces systematic changes in generative distributions.
  Across two architectures, fine-tuned models show stable, context-general shifts in next-token probability distributions, including increased probability assigned to negative and threat-related interpretations in open-ended language tasks. These effects generalize beyond training contexts and are detectable in qualitative completions, psychometric-style evaluations, and quantitative distributional metrics such as Jensen-Shannon divergence.
  Induced behavioral profiles also show partial specificity. Models optimized for different behavioral patterns exhibit dissociable response tendencies across evaluation probes, suggesting that structured behavioral training produces differentiated policy-level biases rather than generic distributional skew.
  We interpret these findings as evidence that consistent behavioral optimization in LLMs can generate stable behavioral and distributional patterns consistent with altered latent priors, linking action selection and language generation. More broadly, the results support a view of LLMs as policy-based systems in which behavioral constraints shape emergent representational structure, highlighting their potential as controlled testbeds for studying the relationship between behavior, interpretation, and generative language in computational models of cognition.

</details>

*Pranshu Rastogi,Madhav Mathur,Ramaneswaran S,Kshitij Mohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文针对社交媒体滥用评论检测问题，提出了一种结合语言预处理和多模型集成的技术方案，旨在最小化将正常评论误判为滥用内容的假阳性率，从而在保障用户安全的同时维护言论自由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交媒体的普及，用户生成内容中存在大量滥用性和有害性内容，因此需要开发有效的滥用评论检测系统。

Method: 采用基于语言学的预处理方法，并结合多种模型的集成学习方案。

Result: 通过大量实验验证，所提出的流水线在保持低误报率方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个最小化误报率的管道，在检测滥用评论的同时不损害言论自由，为社交媒体安全环境建设提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: In recent years social media has become an increasingly popular tool for communication. People use it to share their ideas, exchange information, and discuss thoughts. Given its prevalence and widespread reach, social media must remain a safe space for people. Content generated on social media can be abusive and it has become increasingly important to detect such content. In this paper, we use a language-based preprocessing and an ensemble of several models and analyze their performance of abusive comment detection. Through extensive experimentation, we propose a pipeline that minimizes the false-positive rate (marking non-abusive as abusive) so that these systems can detect abusive comments without undermining the freedom of expression.

</details>


### [41] [Unified Data Selection for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22389)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yubo Ma,Chengpeng Li,Fengbin Zhu,Yiyao Yu,Keqin Bao,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HES, a novel training-free metric measuring reasoning quality via top-token entropy, effectively identifies high-quality reasoning samples across SFT, RFT, and RL paradigms, significantly reducing computational overhead while maintaining or improving performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Training LLMs for complex long-CoT reasoning is bottlenecked by massive high-quality reasoning data requirements. Existing methods are computationally expensive or fail to reliably distinguish high- from low-quality reasoning samples.

Method: High-Entropy Sum (HES) - training-free metric summing entropy of top 0.5% highest-entropy tokens per reasoning sample. Validated across SFT, RFT, and RL training paradigms.

Result: In SFT, top 20% HES-ranked data matches full-dataset performance; lowest-HES data degrades performance. In RFT and RL, HES-based approaches significantly outperform baseline methods.

Conclusion: HES established as a robust, training-free metric enabling unified, effective, and efficient method for developing advanced LLM reasoning capabilities.

Abstract: Effectively training Large Language Models (LLMs) for complex, long-CoT reasoning is often bottlenecked by the need for massive high-quality reasoning data. Existing methods are either computationally expensive or fail to reliably distinguish high- from low-quality reasoning samples. To address this, we propose High-Entropy Sum (HES), a training-free metric that quantifies reasoning quality by summing only the entropy of the top (e.g., 0.5\%) highest-entropy tokens in each reasoning sample. We validate HES across three mainstream training paradigms: Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT), Rejection Fine-tuning (RFT), and Reinforcement Learning (RL), with extensive results demonstrating its consistent effectiveness and significantly reduced computational overhead. In SFT, training on the top 20\% HES-ranked data matches full-dataset performance, while using the lowest-HES data degrades it. In RFT, our HES-based training approach significantly outperforms baseline methods. In RL, HES-selected successful trajectories enable the model to learn strong reasoning patterns, significantly surpassing other compared methods. Our findings establish HES as a robust, training-free metric that enables a unified, effective, and efficient method for developing advanced reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [42] [DeferMem: Query-Time Evidence Distillation via Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term Memory QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22411)
*Jianing Yin,Tan Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeferMem框架解决LLM智能体长时记忆问答问题，通过在查询时进行高召回候选检索和查询条件下的证据精馏来解耦记忆处理。核心贡献是DistillPO强化学习算法，将证据精炼建模为结构化动作（消息选择+证据重写），配合分解门控奖励和结构对齐优势估计优化。在两个基准上以最高准确率和最快速度超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统在查询前预处理记忆，基于相似度而非查询效用检索，导致下游答案生成器需要去噪检索结果并重建证据。

Method: 提出了DeferMem框架和DistillPO强化学习算法。DeferMem采用轻量级段链接结构组织原始历史，在查询时检索宽候选。DistillPO将检索后证据精炼建模为结构化动作（消息选择+证据重写），通过分解门控奖励管道和结构对齐的优势估计来优化。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S数据集上，DeferMem超越强基线方法，以最快运行速度和零商业API token成本实现了最高QA准确率。

Conclusion: DeferMem在长记忆问答任务中实现了SOTA性能，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上实现了最高准确率，同时保持了最快的运行速度和零商业API成本，证明了延迟+精炼两阶段框架的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents still struggle with long-term memory question answering, where answer-supporting evidence is often scattered across long conversational histories and buried in substantial irrelevant content. Existing memory systems typically process memory before future queries are known, then retrieve the resulting units based on similarity rather than their utility for answering the query. This workflow leaves downstream answerers to denoise retrieved candidates and reconstruct query-specific evidence. We present DeferMem, a long-term memory framework that decouples this problem into high-recall candidate retrieval and query-conditioned evidence distillation. DeferMem uses a lightweight segment-link structure to organize raw history and retrieve broad candidates at query time. It then applies a memory distiller trained with DistillPO, our reinforcement learning algorithm for distilling the high-recall but highly noisy candidates into a set of faithful, self-contained, and query-conditioned evidence. DistillPO formulates post-retrieval evidence distillation as a structured action comprising message selection and evidence rewriting. It optimizes this action with a decomposed-and-gated reward pipeline and structure-aligned advantage assignment, gating reward components from validity to quality checks while exposing task-level correctness feedback early and assigning each reward to its responsible output span. On LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S, DeferMem surpasses strong baselines in QA accuracy and memory-system efficiency, achieving the highest QA accuracy with the fastest runtime and zero commercial-API token cost for memory operations.

</details>


### [43] [Assisted Counterspeech Writing at the Crossroads of Hate Speech and Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22435)
*Genoveffa Martone,Helena Bonaldi,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探索了在仇恨言论与错误信息共存场景下使用LLM辅助生成反驳语音（CS）的有效性。通过对比三种知识驱动策略（事实核查驱动、NGO驱动、混合驱动），结合23位专家修订评估，发现LLM可生成约40%合格的CS，而专家修订能显著提升质量。混合策略表现最优，有效结合了事实纠正、刻板印象缓解和同理心参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论和错误信息经常在网上共同出现，放大偏见和极化现象。先前研究分别处理这两种现象，缺乏在两者共存情境下的反驳语音（CS）生成研究。

Method: 测试了三种知识驱动的生成策略：基于事实核查者指南和文章的策略、基于NGO指南和报告的策略，以及混合两者。结合23位专家修订的CS，使用人工和自动指标进行评估。

Result: LLM在40%的情况下能生成 adequate CS；专家修订显著提升了自然性、详尽性和指南遵从度；混合策略在众包评估中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 混合策略在众包评估中表现最佳，平衡了事实纠正、刻板印象缓解和同理心参与。研究发布了带有专家验证的CS和支持知识的仇恨言论与错误信息数据集。

Abstract: Hate speech and misinformation frequently co-occur online, amplifying prejudice and polarization. Given their scale, using Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist expert counterspeech (CS) writing has gained interest, yet prior work has addressed these phenomena separately. We bridge this gap by studying CS generation in contexts where both hate and misinformation co-occur. We test three knowledge-driven generation strategies: first we prompt an LLM with fact-checkers' guidelines and fact-checking articles; secondly, with NGOs' guidelines and reports; thirdly, we create a mixed strategy that combines guidelines and documents from both. 23 experts revise the generated CS, which are assessed via human and automatic metrics. While LLMs produce adequate CS in 40% of cases, expert edits substantially improve naturalness, exhaustiveness, and adherence to guidelines. Based on the post-edited CS, the mixed strategy proves to be the most effective in crowdsourcing evaluation, pairing strong factual correction with stereotype mitigation and empathetic engagement. We release a dataset of hateful and misinformed claims with expert-verified CS and supporting knowledge.

</details>


### [44] [Cohesion-6K: An Arabic Dataset for Analyzing Social Cohesion and Conflict in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22447)
*Aisha Ali Al-Athba,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发布Cohesion-6K数据集，包含6000条关于巴以问题的阿拉伯语Facebook帖子，采用人机协作标注为5个凝聚力相关类别。研究发现冲突性帖子互动量显著高于调解性帖子，揭示分裂话语更易获得关注的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注显性毒性检测，而社会凝聚力的微妙动态——即分裂与统一叙事之间的互动——在计算层面仍缺乏充分探索。

Method: 结合专家人工判断与模型辅助预标注，由训练有素的标注员验证。采用Cohen's kappa评估标注一致性。数据集涵盖6000条阿拉伯语Facebook帖子，分类为5类话语类别：冲突、调解、社区参与、支持性互动和共同价值观。

Result: 冲突导向帖子的用户互动量是调解导向帖子的2-4倍（p < 0.01），证实了分裂性话语在阿拉伯语社交媒体空间中倾向于获得不成比例的可见度。

Conclusion: Cohesion-6K数据集提供了研究在线话语凝聚力的透明且可复现的资源，支持计算社会科学、数字传播和阿拉伯语NLP的未来研究。

Abstract: The study of online discourse has become central to understanding societal polarization. While much research has focused on detecting overt toxicity, the subtle dynamics of social cohesion, meaning the interaction between divisive and unifying narratives, remain computationally underexplored (Bail, 2021; Gonzalez-Bailon and Lelkes, 2023). This paper presents Cohesion-6K, a manually and ChatGPT-assisted annotated dataset of six thousand Arabic public Facebook posts related to the Israeli Occupation of Palestine. Each post is assigned to one of five discourse categories that represent a continuum from conflict to cohesion: Conflict, Resolution, Community Engagement, Supportive Interactions, and Shared Values. The annotation process combines expert human judgment with model-assisted pre-labeling verified by trained annotators, achieving substantial inter-annotator agreement (Cohens kappa = 0.85). Quantitative analysis reveals a consistent engagement gap, where conflict-oriented posts receive between two and four times more user interaction than resolution-oriented ones (p < 0.01). This pattern illustrates how divisive discourse tends to attract disproportionate visibility in Arabic social media spaces. Cohesion-6K provides a transparent and reproducible resource for the study of online cohesion and polarization. The dataset, annotation guidelines, and preprocessing code will be released for research use under an open license, supporting future work in computational social science, digital communication, and Arabic natural language processing.

</details>


### [45] [From Correlation to Cause: A Five-Stage Methodology for Feature Analysis in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22462)
*Caleb Munigety*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出因果特征分析的五阶段方法论，在GPT-2的IOI任务上发现：稀疏自编码器检测到的高选择性特征仅部分因果，消融15个特征后模型准确率仅下降2%；选择性特征与因果贡献呈负相关(r=-0.56)；分布偏移下特征消融效果退化；成本优化后监控成本从$1000降至$8.96/千次查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和验证 transformer 语言模型中因果特征的存在性、作用机制及其实际部署价值。当前方法多依赖相关性分析（如线性探针），但缺乏因果验证，且未充分考虑特征检测能力与因果贡献之间的差异。

Method: 采用五阶段因果特征分析方法：(1) 探针设计用于构建因果分析工具；(2) 特征提取通过稀疏自编码器(SAE)恢复每名称选择性特征；(3) 因果验证通过激活 patching 和特征消融测试因果性；(4) 鲁棒性测试在三种分布偏移下评估迁移性和因果效应稳定性；(5) 部署集成基于成本模型优化监控配置。在 GPT-2 small 的 IOI 任务上进行了端到端验证。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 层9头9单独即可实现 +1.02 的恢复效果；(2) SAE 恢复了效应量为30-50激活单位的选择性特征；(3) 消融15个选择性特征后模型在98%提示上仍准确，表明特征仅为部分因果；(4) 15个选择性特征仅解释31%方差（vs SAE的99.7%），选择性比例与因果力呈负相关(r=-0.56)；(5) 分布偏移下电路迁移良好但特征消融效果显著下降；(6) 最优监控配置成本为$8.96/千次查询，较$1000基线节省99.1%。

Conclusion: 单一方法无法揭示全部发现：稀疏自编码器可高精度检测特征但因果贡献有限；激活 patching 可定位功能但难以评估鲁棒性；选择性特征的反相关现象表明检测到的特征不一定是因果驱动的；部署阶段的成本约束进一步限定了最优监控策略。该方法论框架为理解 transformer 内部机制提供了系统化路径。

Abstract: We propose a five-stage methodology for causal feature analysis in transformer language models (probe design, feature extraction, causal validation, robustness testing, and deployment integration) and demonstrate it end-to-end on GPT-2 small performing the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. Activation patching recovers the canonical IOI circuit (layer-9 head 9 alone gives recovery +1.02). A sparse autoencoder recovers per-name selective features with effect sizes of 30 to 50 activation units. Causal validation finds these features specifically but only partially causal: ablating fifteen of them leaves the model accurate on 98% of prompts. Two NLA-inspired evaluations strengthen this picture: the fifteen selective features explain only 31% of activation variance versus the SAE's 99.7%, and selectivity ratio anticorrelates with causal force (r = -0.56). Robustness testing under three distribution shifts finds that the circuit transfers cleanly but feature ablation effects degrade substantially, exposing a gap between detection robustness and causal robustness. A cost-based deployment evaluation (assumed $50/FN, $0.42/FP, 2% error rate) finds an optimal monitor configuration yielding $8.96 per 1000 queries against a $1000 baseline, a 99.1% saving. Optimal composition strategy varies with cost ratio and base rate. The conjunction of stages produces findings no single stage would.

</details>

### [46] [Faster Completion, Less Learning: Generative AI Reduced Study Time on Math Problems and the Knowledge They Build](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21629)
*Sina Rismanchian,Hasan Uzun,Jeffrey Matayoshi,Eric Cosyn,Eyad Kurd-Misto*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过十年来320万条ALEKS学习互动记录和placement-assessment数据，采用准实验设计（文本题vs图形题），发现ChatGPT发布后大学生在高AI敏感问题上的学习时间下降26.9%，高中生下降31.3%。在监考条件下时间差异消失，但监考后保留测试的正确率 odds 下降25%，非监考测试则显示相反的大幅增加。研究得出结论：生成式AI已改变学生学习方式和知识构建，表现为"认知投降"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解决一个重要悖论：自我报告调查显示学生对AI的使用变化不大，但小规模行为研究虽然发现广泛使用却缺乏足够规模和时长来测量学习后果。作者试图通过大规模纵向数据来明确回答：AI如何改变学生的学习过程，以及这对持久学习成果有何影响。

Method: 采用准实验设计，利用课程内AI敏感性的差异：可转录为AI提示的文本应用题作为处理组，需要交互式平台操作的图形题作为对照组。时间分析使用十年面板数据（320万条ALEKS互动），保留率分析使用ALEKS PPL placement数据，通过logistic固定效应模型分析随机分配的监考保留测试项目。

Result: ChatGPT发布后，大学生在AI敏感问题上的学习时间每季度下降2.8%，累计11季度下降26.9%；高中生下降31.3%，初中生9.0%，五年级学生无显著变化。监考条件下时间差异完全消失。在监考保留测试中正确率的odds累计下降25%；而非监考评估则显示大幅相反方向的增加。

Conclusion: 生成式AI已大规模改变学生学习方式和知识构建——作者将此定义为"认知投降"（cognitive surrender）。监考/非监考结果的不一致表明，学生并未真正提高学习效率，而是在借助AI完成任务后无法独立记忆和应用知识。该发现对教育研究、评估治理和AI政策具有直接启示意义。

Abstract: How much have students' ordinary learning processes shifted in response to generative AI, and how does that affect their durable learning outcomes? Self-report surveys show little change, while small-scale behavioral studies report widespread AI use without the scale or duration to measure learning consequences. We address both questions using a ten-year panel of $3.2$ million ALEKS learning interactions for the time-on-task analysis, complemented by ALEKS PPL placement-assessment data for the proctoring and retention analyses, with a quasi-experimental design exploiting within-curriculum variation in AI susceptibility: text-based word problems transcribable into AI prompts serve as the treated group; graph-based problems requiring interactive platform manipulation as the comparison. Learning time on AI-susceptible problems declines $2.8\%$ per quarter among college students after ChatGPT's release, cumulating to $26.9\%$ over eleven quarters; high-schoolers show $31.3\%$, middle-schoolers $9.0\%$, and Grade 5 students no detectable change. The divergence vanishes entirely under proctoring for college students, making general efficiency gains unlikely. Logistic fixed-effects models on randomly assigned proctored retention items yield a $25\%$ cumulative decline in odds of correct response; the same estimator on non-proctored assessment produces a large opposite-signed increase -- inconsistent with any platform, cohort, or curriculum explanation. These results are among the first large-scale behavioral and outcome evidence that generative AI has altered how students study and the knowledge they build -- the population-level indicator of \emph{cognitive surrender}, with direct implications for educational research, assessment governance, and AI policy.

</details>


### [47] [Barriers to Evidence in AI-Related Cases and the Privatization of Proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21816)
*Sarah H. Cen,Hannah Ismael,Lucia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper examines how asymmetries in access, resources, and expertise create barriers to evidence in AI-related litigation, identifying seven recurring sources of asymmetry and introducing the concept of "privatization of proof." It proposes a three-part test to resolve AI access disputes, with the key insight that different types of access can be fungible substitutes for each other.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Evidence is increasingly difficult to obtain in AI-related disputes because critical facts are hidden within proprietary models, platform logs, and protected databases, creating asymmetric barriers that prevent meritorious cases from proceeding.

Method: The paper analyzes past and ongoing cases to identify recurring patterns, characterizes seven sources of asymmetry, and develops a legal test for AI access disputes.

Result: Seven recurring sources of asymmetry were identified (access to models, data, documentation, logs, expertise, compute, and infrastructure), and the concept of "privatization of proof" was characterized. Additionally, different types of access can be fungible, meaning alternative forms of access may yield functionally equivalent information.

Conclusion: The paper proposes a three-part test for resolving AI access disputes in litigation, incorporating proportionality and reasonable alternatives, with the cause of action serving as a baseline for access.

Abstract: Evidence lies at the core of litigation, but it is increasingly difficult to obtain in AI-related disputes. Even when a claimant's position has merit, cases are often settled or dismissed because decisive facts are hidden inside proprietary models, platform logs, and protected databases. Grounding our discussion in past and ongoing cases, we investigate how asymmetries in access, resources, and expertise can create significant barriers to evidence in AI-related cases. We show how developers and deployers resist disclosure through various strategies challenging the value of the evidence to the requesting party and the cost of evidence production. From these patterns we identify seven recurring sources of asymmetry -- access to models, data, documentation, logs, expertise, compute, and infrastructure -- that reflect a broader pattern that we call the privatization of proof: when control over proof falls in the hands of private actors that can demand justification for access while ensuring that justification remains out of reach. We further argue that different types of access can be fungible: in the absence of a certain type of access (e.g., to model internals), one may be able to use alternative forms of access (e.g., sufficient compute, query access, and access to user logs) and to obtain a functionally equivalent amount of information. We propose a three-part test that can help resolve AI access disputes in litigation, drawing on concepts such as proportionality and reasonable alternatives. Our test relies on a few observations, including that the cause of action can provide a baseline for access.

</details>


### [48] [Healthcare LLM Benchmarks Are Only as Good as Their Explicit Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22612)
*Naveen Raman,Santiago Cortes-Gomez,Mateo Dulce Rubio,Fei Fang,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文指出医疗AI评估中基准测试与实际部署效果之间存在差距，其根源在于基准测试无法揭示用户交互相关的隐性假设。作者将假设分为任务假设（可从对话数据测试）和结果假设（需结果数据和行为研究测试），并通过回顾性分析医疗RCT发现两类gap大致相等。提出BenchmarkCards文档和分阶段评估流程作为解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Benchmarks are insufficient for predicting deployment performance in healthcare AI. The evaluation-deployment gap exists because benchmarks cannot surface implicit assumptions about user-model interactions.

Method: The authors propose a classification framework dividing assumptions into task assumptions (testable from conversation data alone) and outcome assumptions (requiring outcome data and behavioral studies). They retrospectively analyze a healthcare RCT to validate this framework.

Result: Retrospective analysis of a healthcare RCT reveals the gap naturally separates into task and outcome gaps of roughly equal size.

Conclusion: The evaluation-deployment gap in healthcare AI arises from implicit assumptions that benchmarks cannot capture. The authors propose BenchmarkCards and staged evaluation as solutions to systematically document and test these assumptions.

Abstract: Benchmarks are necessary for healthcare evaluation, but are not sufficient for predicting deployment performance. Our position is that the evaluation--deployment gap arises not because of poorly designed benchmarks, but from implicit assumptions about how users interact with models that cannot be surfaced from benchmarks alone. To make this precise, we propose a classification of assumptions into two categories: task, which can be tested from conversation data alone, and outcome, which requires outcome data and behavioral studies for testing. Critically, outcome assumptions depend on human behavior, something that even well-designed benchmarks cannot directly observe. To demonstrate the operationality of this framework, we retrospectively analyze a healthcare RCT as a case study and find that the gap naturally separates into task and outcome gaps of roughly equal size. To address this, we make two contributions: first, we propose BenchmarkCards, an artifact that documents assumptions, and second, we propose staged evaluation, a procedure that systematically tests assumptions and evaluates performance.

</details>


### [49] [The efficiency-gain illusion: People underestimate the rate of AI use and overestimate its benefits on simple tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22687)
*Sunny Yu,Myra Cheng,Ahmad Jabbar,Ilia Sucholutsky,Katherine M. Collins,Dan Jurafsky,Robert D. Hawkins*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 尽管AI助手被用于简单任务（如算术、拼写检查），但人们常常在AI并不节省时间的情况下仍选择使用它。研究发现人们存在两种系统性偏差：低估自身AI使用频率，以及高估AI带来的时间节省。这种偏差形成正向反馈循环，可能导致过度依赖AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As AI assistance becomes ubiquitous for simple tasks, a fundamental question emerges: does AI assistance actually save users time and effort? The researchers seek to understand whether people's reliance on AI for cognitively simple tasks is well-calibrated to actual efficiency gains.

Method: Three pre-registered user studies with N = 2691 participants, investigating cognitive simple tasks.

Result: People frequently use AI even when inefficient; two systematic miscalibrations identified: (1) self-estimate miscalibration—people believe they use AI less than they actually do; (2) efficiency-gain illusions—people overestimate time/effort savings from AI. A session-level carryover effect was also found: prior AI use leads to increased subsequent AI adoption and entrenches miscalibration about time savings.

Conclusion: People exhibit systematic miscalibration in AI use decisions, with two distinct biases: underestimating their own AI usage and overestimating time savings. This creates a risk of overreliance feedback loops where initial AI use perpetuates further adoption regardless of actual utility. The findings highlight critical mechanisms underlying human-AI decision-making and the potential risks of uncritical AI adoption.

Abstract: People are increasingly turning to AI assistance for simple tasks, e.g., arithmetic, spell-check, and answering simple questions. But does AI assistance actually save users time and effort? We investigate people's propensity to use AI for cognitively simple tasks and assess whether their reliance is well-calibrated. Across three pre-registered user studies (N = 2691), we find that people frequently choose to use AI even when doing so is inefficient (i.e. provides no meaningful time or effort savings). We identify systematic miscalibration at two levels: (1) a self-estimate miscalibration where people on average believe that they are using AI less than they actually are, and (2) efficiency-gain illusions where people overestimate how much time and effort savings AI use affords. We also identify a session-level carryover effect where a participant's prior AI use leads to further AI adoption and entrenches their miscalibration about time savings. Our results shed light on the mechanisms and biases underlying people's choice of whether to use AI as well as the risk of an overreliance feedback loop.

</details>

### [50] [Bridging the Cold-Start Gap: LLM-Powered Synthetic Data Generation for Natural Language Search at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21812)
*Wendy Ran Wei,Hao Li,Weiwei Guo,Xiaowei Liu,Xueyin Chen,Dillon Davis,Malay Haldar,Soumyadip Banerjee,Kedar Bellare,Huiji Gao,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Airbnb使用大语言模型（LLM）生成合成查询和标签，解决自然语言搜索系统的冷启动问题。通过结合对比房源对与种子查询，平衡真实性和多样性；通过对比生成和Virtual Judge方法生成标签。实验表明该方法在查询长度和属性分布上均显著优于基线，并已部署生产管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言搜索系统部署面临冷启动挑战：缺乏真实用户查询用于学习语言模式，缺乏相关标签用于训练排序模型。

Method: 提出基于LLM的合成查询和标签生成框架。查询生成：结合预订会话中的对比房源对与用户研究的种子查询，平衡真实性和多样性，支持冷启动到温启动的过渡。标签生成：引入对比生成方法构造主题性标签，以及Virtual Judge(VJ)标注方法实现更广覆盖。

Result: 对比实验结果：(1)查询长度：InPars基线KL散度12.03，种子引导方法达0.66，提升7.5倍；(2)属性类型分布：KL散度0.04，优于种子查询(0.09)；(3)评估难度：no-seed基线成对准确率97%，本方法79%，产生更具区分性的评估样本。

Conclusion: 已部署生产管道，每日生成合成样本用于基于嵌入的检索和排序评估。

Abstract: Deploying natural language search systems presents a critical cold-start challenge: no real user queries to learn linguistic patterns, and no relevance labels to train ranking models. We present a framework for generating synthetic queries and labels using large language models (LLMs), powering model training and evaluation for Airbnb's natural language search.
  For query generation, we combine contrastive listing pairs from booking sessions with seed queries from user research to balance realism and diversity, enabling a cold-to-warm start transition as real user data becomes available. For label generation, we introduce contrastive generation that produces topicality labels by construction, and Virtual Judge (VJ) labeling for broader coverage.
  We compare our approach against a no-seed contrastive baseline and an InPars-style baseline. For query length, the InPars baseline produces verbose queries with KL divergence of 12.03 vs. real users; our seed-guided approach achieves 0.66, a 7.5x improvement. For attribute type distributions, our approach achieves the lowest KL divergence (0.04), outperforming even seed queries (0.09). Experiments show our approach produces harder evaluation examples than the no-seed baseline (79% vs. 97% pairwise accuracy), providing discriminative signal for model improvement. We deploy production pipelines generating synthetic examples daily for embedding-based retrieval and ranking evaluation.

</details>


### [51] [Reinforced Preference Optimization for Reasoning-Augmented Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21967)
*Jingtong Gao,Zeyu Song,Chi Lu,Xiaopeng Li,Derong Xu,Maolin Wang,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai,Qingpeng Cai,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RPORec框架，通过强化偏好优化统一LLM推理能力与专用推荐头(Rechead)，采用推理增强推荐建模和基于可验证奖励的强化学习微调两阶段策略，有效解决了现有方法中推理与推荐目标对齐不足的问题，显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的推荐方法在整合时存在结构断裂，且难以将自由形式生成转化为精确物品预测，未能充分将LLM推理过程与推荐特定目标对齐。

Method: 提出RPORec框架，包含两个阶段：(1)推理增强推荐建模：生成高质量思维链(CoT)推理作为辅助知识，引导Rechead学习推荐特定表示；(2)高级推理优化与对齐：训练好的Rechead产生可验证奖励，通过强化学习微调LLM骨干，提升推理质量、结构一致性和任务相关性。

Result: 在公开基准和大规模在线部署中，RPORec一致性地超越现有最先进的LLM推荐方法。

Conclusion: RPORec框架通过两阶段设计有效统一了LLM推理能力与专用推荐头，在公开基准和大规模在线部署中均优于现有最先进的LLM推荐方法。

Abstract: Recommender systems are critical for delivering personalized content across digital platforms, and recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to enhance them with richer world knowledge and explicit reasoning capabilities. With the help of reasoning knowledge, recommendations can better infer users' underlying intents, adapt to evolving preferences, and leverage semantic relationships for improved accuracy and interpretability. However, existing reasoning-based recommendation methods often fail to fully align the LLM's reasoning process with recommendation-specific objectives due to structural disruption during integration and difficulties in translating free-form generation into accurate item predictions. In this paper, we introduce RPORec, a reinforced preference optimization framework that unifies an LLM backbone's reasoning ability with a dedicated recommendation head (Rechead) for precise item retrieval. RPORec comprises two stages: (1) Reasoning-Augmented Recommendation Modeling, where high-quality Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning is generated and used as auxiliary knowledge to guide the Rechead in learning recommendation-specific representations; and (2) Advanced Reasoning Refinement and Alignment, in which the trained Rechead produces verifiable rewards to fine-tune the LLM backbone via reinforcement learning, enhancing reasoning quality, structural consistency, and task relevance. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and large-scale online deployments show that RPORec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-based recommendation methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of reasoning-augmented recommendation modeling in real-world systems.

</details>


### [52] [LLM Retrieval for Stable and Predictable Ad Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21969)
*Vinodh Kumar Sunkara,Satheeshkumar Karuppusamy,Hangjun Xu,Sai Deepika Regani,Kshitij Gupta,Gaby Nahum,Sneha Iyer,Jean-Baptiste Fiot,Yinglong Guo,Xiaowen Guo,Atul Jangra,Yucheng Liu,Jinghao Yan,Vijay Pappu,Benjamin Schulte,Deepak Chandra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于微调大语言模型(LLM)的语义候选生成框架，用于提升广告推荐系统的预测稳定性和可预测性。框架从广告创意中提取层级语义属性获取LLM表示，基于图扩展确保检索结果涵盖广告的语义变体，保证小幅创意变体产生一致且可解释的投放效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional ads recommendation systems focus on prediction accuracy (click/conversion events) using metrics like recall and NDCG. However, with the hyper-growth of ads inventory and generative AI technologies, prediction stability and predictability are becoming increasingly critical. The lack of stability leads to problems like repeatability, cold start, and under-exploration that advertisers can perceive.

Method: The approach extracts hierarchical semantic attributes from ad creatives to obtain LLM representations, which serve as the foundation for graph-based expansion. This ensures retrieved candidates encapsulate semantic variants of an ad, guaranteeing consistent and explainable delivery results for small creative variants.

Result: Significant improvements across both offline and online A/B experiments in terms of predictability and traditional performance metrics.

Conclusion: LLM-based semantic candidate generation framework demonstrates significant improvements in both offline and online A/B experiments. The approach is generalizable and can be applied to any large-scale recommendation and retrieval systems.

Abstract: Traditional ads recommendation systems have primarily focused on optimizing for prediction accuracy of click or conversion events using canonical metrics such as recall or normalized discounted cumulative gain (NDCG). With the hyper-growth of ads inventory and liquidity with generative AI technologies, the prediction stability and predictability is becoming increasingly critical. Intuitively, prediction stability and predictability can be defined to quantify system robustness with respect to minor/noisy input (ads, creatives) perturbations, the lack of which could lead to advertiser perceivable problems such as repeatability, cold start and under-exploration. In this paper, we introduce a new evaluation framework for quantifying stability and predictability of an ads recommender system, and present an online validated semantic candidate generation framework powered by fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) that showed significant improvement along these metrics by fundamentally improving the semantic-awareness of the system. The approach extracts hierarchical semantic attributes from ad creatives to obtain LLM representations, which serve as the foundation for graph-based expansion, ensuring the retrieved candidates encapsulate semantic variants of an ad, guaranteeing that small creative variants from the advertiser yield consistent and explainable delivery results to the user. We tested this LLM ads retrieval framework in a large-scale industrial ads recommendation system, demonstrating significant improvements across offline and online A/B experiments, showcasing gains in both predictability and traditional performance metrics. Although evaluated in the ads stack, this is a general framework that can be applied broadly to any large-scale recommendation and retrieval systems facing similar scaling and predictability challenges.

</details>


### [53] [Generative Conversational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21987)
*Sixiao Zhang,Mingrui Liu,Cheng Long*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This paper proposes a fully generative conversational recommender system that unifies recommendation and dialog generation within a single autoregressive framework. By representing items as discrete semantic IDs and using a structured generation paradigm that factorizes the decision process, the method achieves significant recommendation improvements (up to 29% Recall@1 gain) while maintaining dialog quality.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing conversational recommender systems either decouple recommendation from dialog generation or rely on retrieval-based pipelines, limiting integration between recommendation and response generation, leading to suboptimal modeling of user intent

Method: Represent items as discrete semantic IDs; structured generation paradigm that factorizes conversational recommendation into interdependent decisions (predict response intent → predict recommendation target → generate response); next-token modeling for joint prediction; constrained decoding for faithful item generation

Result: Achieves up to 29% Recall@1 improvement over strong baselines while maintaining competitive dialog quality

Conclusion: End-to-end optimization with coherent dependency structure and faithful item generation via constrained decoding

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems aim to provide personalized recommendations via natural language interactions. However, existing approaches either decouple recommendation from dialog generation or rely on retrieval-based pipelines, limiting the integration between recommendation and response generation and leading to suboptimal modeling of user intent. In this paper, we propose a fully generative conversational recommender system that unifies recommendation and dialog generation within a single autoregressive framework. Our approach represents items as discrete semantic IDs and integrates them directly into the generation process, enabling joint prediction of items and responses via next-token modeling. We further introduce a structured generation paradigm that factorizes conversational recommendation into a sequence of interdependent decisions, where the model first predicts the response intent and the recommendation target, and then generates the response conditioned on them. This design enables end-to-end optimization, enforces a more coherent dependency structure, and supports faithful item generation via constrained decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently improves recommendation performance, achieving gains of up to 29% on Recall@1 over strong baselines, while maintaining competitive dialog quality.

</details>


### [54] [Integrating Chain-of-Thought into Generative Retrieval: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22358)
*Wenhao Zhang,Ruihao Yu,Yi Bai,Zhumin Chen,Pengjie Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ThinkGR将思维链（CoT）整合进生成式检索，通过混合解码策略和两阶段训练实现迭代推理与检索，在多跳检索任务上实现SOTA，验证了显式推理能力对复杂检索任务的提升作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索方法直接将查询映射到文档标识符，缺少中间推理过程，导致处理需要多步推理的复杂查询时效果受限。

Method: ThinkGR采用混合解码策略，在无约束思维生成与受限docid解码之间动态切换；并采用两阶段训练方法，第一阶段通过监督微调对齐思维-检索模式，第二阶段通过检索导向的强化学习优化思维质量。

Result: 在四个多跳检索基准数据集上实验表明，ThinkGR达到最优性能，平均提升+6.86%。

Conclusion: ThinkGR为生成式检索领域开辟了新方向，证明显式推理能力可显著提升复杂查询场景下的检索性能，对需要多跳推理的检索任务具有重要的实践意义。

Abstract: While generative retrieval (GR) demonstrates competitive performance on standard retrieval benchmarks, existing approaches directly map queries to document identifiers (docids) without intermediate deliberation, limiting their effectiveness for complex queries that require multi-step reasoning. As a preliminary study on integrating chain-of-thought (CoT) into generative retrieval, we introduce ThinkGR, a unified framework that interleaves CoT with docid generation, enabling iterative thinking and retrieval within a single generative process. To bridge the gap between free-form thought generation and structured retrieval targets, we design (1) a hybrid decoding strategy that dynamically switches between unconstrained thought generation and constrained docid decoding, and (2) a two-phase training approach that first aligns thought-retrieval patterns through supervised fine-tuning, then optimizes thought quality via retrieval-grounded reinforcement learning. Experiments on four multi-hop retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkGR achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average improvement of +6.86\%. Our work opens new avenues for enhancing generative retrieval with explicit deliberation capabilities, with promising implications for retrieval tasks requiring complex reasoning.

</details>


### [55] [Diversed Model Discovery via Structured Table Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22766)
*Zhengyuan Dong,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出StructuredSemanticSearch框架，针对现有模型搜索系统过度依赖语义相似性导致结果同质化的问题，通过结构化表格驱动的检索方法，结合表格发现算子（unionability、joinability、keyword search）和方向感知集成技术，实现更优的证据覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing model search systems rely predominantly on semantic similarity over text, producing homogeneous result sets and limiting exploration of alternatives. The authors argue model search is inherently comparative and requires retrieval over condensed, high-quality evidence from structured tables.

Method: Table-driven model search combining semantic baseline for task alignment with structure-aware pipeline; nugget-based evaluation protocol; orientation-aware table integration for handling partially overlapping/transposed tables.

Result: Structure-aware pipeline achieves improved nugget coverage compared to semantic baseline on 597 model-recommendation queries.

Conclusion: Experiments on 597 model-recommendation queries demonstrate improved nugget coverage for the structure-aware pipeline compared to semantic baseline retrieval.

Abstract: Model cards describe model behavior through a mixture of textual descriptions and structured artifacts, including performance, configuration, and dataset tables. Existing model search systems rely predominantly on semantic similarity over text, which can produce homogeneous result sets and limit exploration of alternatives. We argue that model search is inherently comparative: users want models that are task-aligned yet differentiated in measurable ways. We hypothesize that this balance requires retrieval over condensed, high-quality evidence rather than verbose descriptions, and much of that evidence is concentrated in structured tables. We present StructuredSemanticSearch, a table-driven model search framework built on the ModelTables benchmark. Given a query, StructuredSemanticSearch combines a semantic baseline for task alignment with a structure-aware pipeline that discovers query-related model-card tables using table discovery operators such as unionability, joinability, and keyword search. Retrieved tables are mapped back to model cards under a controlled top-k budget, enabling fair comparison between text-based and table-based retrieval. Beyond retrieval, StructuredSemanticSearch adapts table integration to the model-table domain through orientation-aware integration, producing compact integrated views of tables from partially overlapping and sometimes transposed evidence tables. For evaluation, we introduce a nugget-based, auditable protocol that extracts compact evidence items from model cards, matches queries to condition- or intent-specific nuggets, and measures evidence coverage and diversity over retrieved model-card candidate sets. This protocol also provides a scalable path toward approximate, evidence-based labeling in dynamic model lakes. Experiments on 597 model-recommendation queries show improved nugget coverage for the structure-aware pipeline than semantic baseline

</details>

### [56] [Near-Optimal Generalized Private Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21601)
*Anamay Chaturvedi,Monika Henzinger,Jalaj Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义阈值机制（GTM），用于解决差分隐私下的广义私人测试问题，实现了纯ε-DP保证、近似最优的准确性/样本复杂度，并成功将持续观测设置约化为批量设置，支持自适应阈值选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏向量技术（SVT）无法处理机制成功概率的连续变化和多阈值设定问题，且先前工作指出需要解决自适应阈值选择的挑战，以支持差分隐私超参数优化等应用场景。

Method: 提出广义阈值机制（GTM），通过精心设计的拒绝阈值p̄_t公式和样本复杂度上界分析，实现对任意(ε_t, δ_t)-DP机制的纯ε-DP保证。

Result: GTM在pure ε-DP保证下，对于任意(ε_t, δ_t)-DP机制序列均有效；通过下界证明展示了准确性和样本复杂度的近似最优性；成功将持续观测（CO）设置约化为批量设置，获得了首个针对多种最大化问题的DP-CO算法。

Conclusion: 提出的GTM机制在准确性和样本复杂度上接近最优，为差分隐私的持续观测设置提供了首个通用黑盒约化方法，并支持自适应阈值选择，具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: In differential privacy (DP), the generalized private testing problem was introduced by Liu and Talwar (STOC 2019). Given a dataset $X \in \mathcal{X}$ and a sequence of black-box $\varepsilon_t$-DP mechanisms $M_t:\mathcal{X}\to\{+1,-1\}$, the analyst must accept the first mechanism whose success probability $p_t=\Pr[M_t(X)=+1]$ exceeds a given threshold $p^*\in(0,1)$, while achieving DP. Accuracy is measured by the gap between $p^*$ and a rejection threshold $\bar{p}$, such that with probability $1-β$ for all $t\geq1$, if $p_t\leq\bar{p}$, then $M_t$ is rejected, and if $p_t\geq p^*$, then it is accepted. This generalizes the standard private testing problem, whose solution, the Sparse Vector Technique, is ubiquitous in DP.
  We introduce the Generalized Thresholding Mechanism (GTM) for generalized private testing. For $\varepsilon>0$ and any sequence of $(\varepsilon_t,δ_t)$-DP mechanisms $M_t$, the GTM is pure $\varepsilon$-DP. For $θ>0$, $γ\in(1,2]$, and $β\in(0,1)$, $\bar{p}_t=\max(p^*/γΛ_t, 1 - γΛ_t(1-p^*))-δ_t/\varepsilon_t$ for $Λ_t=(5t\ln^3(t+2))^{(2+θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}(4/β)^{(3+θ+2/θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}$. With probability $1-β$, the number of evaluations of $M_t$ is at most $O((\ln(t/β)/(γ-1)^2)\max(Λ_t/p^*,(1-p^*)^{-1}))$ for all $t\geq 1$. Our lower bounds prove near-optimality of our accuracy and sample complexity guarantees.
  Via the GTM, we give a black-box reduction for DP optimization from the continual observation (CO) setting to the batch setting. This gives us the first DP-CO algorithms for many maximization problems. Further, the GTM permits an adaptive choice of acceptance thresholds $(p^*_t)_{t\geq1}$, addressing a challenge mentioned in prior work on using generalized private testing for hyperparameter optimization (Papernot and Steinke (ICLR 2022)).

</details>


### [57] [Finding a Solution to the Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv Theorem in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21753)
*Sunghyeon Jo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文将Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理子序列查找问题的算法复杂度从O(n log log log n)优化到确定性线性时间O(n)，核心是提出了一种维护可达和的紧致等差数列表示的线性时间算法来解决素数目标子集和问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理是组合数论中的经典结果，但缺乏高效的算法实现。此前最优算法复杂度为O(n log log log n)，仍有优化空间，且实际应用中需要更快速的确定性算法。

Method: 算法维护可达和的紧致等差数列表示；当两个等差数列相交时，利用有界Frobenius区间的性质将其合并为更长的等差数列，实现足够的增长以覆盖更新成本；当表示包含完整等差数列或覆盖所有非零剩余类时，即可构造性地恢复目标剩余类；最后通过乘法归约将素数算法推广到任意模数。

Result: 提出首个确定性线性时间(O(n))算法，比此前最优的O(n log log log n)算法有显著提升。该算法通过等差数列表示和Frobenius区间合并技术实现了复杂度从对数因子到常数因子的突破。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅解决了Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理的算法实现难题，其等差数列表示和合并技术可能对其他组合优化问题具有借鉴意义，并确立了子集和类问题的算法复杂度下界。

Abstract: The Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv theorem states that every sequence of 2n - 1 integers contains a subsequence of length n whose sum is divisible by n. Choi, Kang, and Lim gave a simple deterministic O(n log n) algorithm for finding such a subsequence, and Leung recently improved this to O(n log log log n). We give a deterministic linear-time algorithm. The core is a linear-time algorithm for the following prime target subset-sum problem: given p - 1 nonzero residues in Z_p and a target residue, find a subset with the prescribed sum. Our algorithm maintains a compact arithmetic-progression representation of reachable sums. When two progressions intersect, a bounded Frobenius interval in their sum allows them to be merged into one longer progression, with enough growth to pay for the update. When the representation either contains a full progression or covers all nonzero residues, the target residue is recovered constructively. The standard multiplicative reduction then extends the prime algorithm to arbitrary moduli.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the classic cake cutting problem in the Robertson-Webb model, with the objective of proportional fairness. We show that any randomized algorithm must use $Ω(n \log n)$ queries.

### [59] [Optimal $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$-Approximation of the Permanent of Positive Semidefinite Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21946)
*Nima Anari,Farzam Ebrahimnejad*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 确定了下Hermitian半正定矩阵永久式在确定多项式时间下的最优近似比。通过构造新的近似上界P̂(A)并证明精确的sandwich不等式 e^(-γn)P̂(A) ≤ per(A) ≤ P̂(A)，其中γ是欧拉-马歇罗尼常数，结合已有的下界硬度结果，证明了确定多项式时间算法的最优指数近似比为 e^((γ+o(1))n)，假设P≠NP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永久式（permanent）计算是组合数学和计算复杂性理论中的核心问题。对于半正定矩阵的永久式，需要确定确定型多项式时间算法能达到的最优近似比。这是一个长期悬而未决的理论问题。

Method: 方法基于Wick积分公式的熵论证。构造了函数Φ(V)——一个关于正定矩阵X的凹最大化问题，定义为：Φ(V)=max_{X≻0}{Σ log(v_i^dagger Xv_i) + log det X - tr X + d}，其中v_i是矩阵V的行向量。通过对Wick公式应用最优输运/熵论证，损失恰好为γ per因子，因为当T~Exp(1)时E[log T] = -γ。

Result: 证明了精确的sandwich不等式：e^(-γn)P̂(A) ≤ per(A) ≤ P̂(A)，其中γ是欧拉-马歇罗尼常数（约0.577）。这给出了确定多项式时间的 e^((γ+ε)n)-近似算法。结合已有的 e^((γ-ε)n)-不可近似性下界，确定最优指数近似比为 e^((γ+o(1))n)。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了半正定矩阵永久式在确定多项式时间下的最优指数近似率。在假设P≠NP的前提下，e^((γ+o(1))n)（其中γ为欧拉-马歇罗尼常数）是确定型算法能达到的精确极限。此结果标志着在矩阵永久式近似这一经典问题上取得了最终答案。

Abstract: We determine, up to lower-order terms in the exponent, the best possible deterministic polynomial-time approximation ratio for the permanent of a Hermitian positive semidefinite matrix. If $A\succeq 0$ has no zero diagonal entry, $d=\operatorname{rank}(A)$, $A=VV^\dagger$ with $V\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times d}$ full column rank, and $v_1,\ldots,v_n$ are the rows of $V$, define \[
  Φ(V)=\max_{X\succ 0}
  \left\{\sum_{i=1}^n \log(v_i^\dagger Xv_i)+\log\det X-\operatorname{tr} X+d\right\},
  \qquad
  \widehat P(A)=e^{Φ(V)}. \] We prove the exact sandwich \[
  e^{-γn}\widehat P(A)\le \operatorname{per}(A)\le \widehat P(A). \] Here $γ$ is the Euler--Mascheroni constant. Since the maximization is concave, this gives a deterministic polynomial-time $e^{(γ+\varepsilon)n}$-approximation for every $\varepsilon>0$. Combined with the previous $e^{(γ-\varepsilon)n}$-hardness of approximation for positive semidefinite permanents, this resolves the optimal exponential approximation ratio for deterministic polynomial-time algorithms as $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$, assuming $\mathrm{P}\ne\mathrm{NP}$. The proof is an entropy argument applied to the standard Wick integral formula for $\operatorname{per}(A)$; the loss is exactly $γ$ per factor because $\mathbb{E}[\log T]=-γ$ for $T\sim\operatorname{Exp}(1)$.
  The result was obtained through interactions with GPT 5.5 Pro Extended: the first author's interaction was one-shot, while the second author's was a separate multi-turn interaction with high-level guidance. Both authors verified the theorem and proof. Codex was used to assemble and typeset the manuscript.

</details>


### [60] [A Coalgebraic Dijkstra Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22149)
*Takahiro Sanada,Yoàv Montacute,Kittiphon Phalakarn,Ichiro Hasuo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文引入coalgebraic shortest path problem (CSPP)作为统一框架，将Dijkstra算法的多种变体（如 widest path、两人对策）以及新问题（如最短二叉树）纳入同一理论体系，并设计了coalgebraic Dijkstra算法。该算法在满足必要且充分条件时可正确求解，且复杂度与经典算法可比。这一工作明确了Dijkstra风格加速技术的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dijkstra算法作为求解最短路问题的经典方法，存在多种变体（如 widest path 问题、两人对策问题），这些变体之间缺乏统一的理论框架。该研究试图建立一个能够涵盖这些问题的泛化模型，以揭示不同优化问题之间的内在联系，并探索Dijkstra风格加速技术在不同问题类型中的适用性边界。

Method: 提出了coalgebraic shortest path problem (CSPP)作为统一框架，并在此框架下设计了coalgebraic Dijkstra算法。该算法的核心是利用余代数结构对状态转移系统进行泛化建模，从而将多种优化问题统一处理。研究者给出了算法正确性的必要且充分条件。复杂度分析表明该算法与经典Dijkstra算法具有可比的渐进复杂度。

Result: 提出了coalgebraic shortest path problem (CSPP)作为统一框架，成功涵盖最短路、 widest path、两人对策等经典变体，并发现了新的应用场景（如最短二叉树问题）。算法在满足必要且充分条件时可正确求解CSPP，且渐进复杂度与经典Dijkstra算法相当。

Conclusion: 该研究为状态转移系统上的优化问题提供了统一的算法框架，通过引入CSPP和coalgebraic Dijkstra算法，既涵盖经典问题又发现新应用场景，且在保持渐进复杂度优势的同时提供了精确的正确性判据。

Abstract: The Dijkstra algorithm is a classical method for solving the shortest path problem on weighted graphs. There are several variations of the Dijkstra algorithm, including algorithms for the widest path problem and for two-player games. In this paper, we introduce the coalgebraic shortest path problem (CSPP), a unifying framework for a broad class of optimization problems on state-transition systems. This framework encompasses not only the aforementioned problems but also new ones such as the shortest binary tree problem. We further present a coalgebraic Dijkstra algorithm for solving the CSPP efficiently under a suitable condition. Our condition is necessary and sufficient for the algorithm to return correct solutions, thereby providing a precise criterion for when Dijkstra-style acceleration is possible. We also show that the proposed algorithm achieves asymptotic complexity comparable to that of the classical Dijkstra algorithm.

</details>


### [61] [The Secretary Problem with a Stochastic Precursor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22653)
*Franziska Eberle,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文创新性地证明预测的到达时间本身即可作为有价值的信号，即使该信号不含任何内容信息。研究将经典的 secretary 问题与一个"前驱信号"(precursor) 结合：该信号保证在最优元素出现前到达，但时间分布随机。核心发现：在随机顺序模型下，单个均匀分布的前驱信号即可将成功率从1/e提升至至少1/2；随信号越晚到达，成功率趋近于1。在对抗顺序模型下（传统模型无法提供保障），足够集中的信号仍可恢复常数级成功保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习增强算法的研究聚焦于预测内容的价值（如数值估计、解方案等），本文旨在探索预测的另一个维度——到达时间的独立价值。特别是针对经典的 secretary 问题，引入"无内容信号"概念，验证时间信息本身能否改善在线决策效果。

Method: 1. 形式化定义"前驱信号"(precursor)：保证在最优元素出现前到达但无内容信息的随机时间信号；2. 分别在随机顺序模型和对抗顺序模型下分析；3. 利用最优停止理论刻画最优策略结构；4. 通过概率分析和界推导证明各场景下的成功概率下界。

Result: 随机顺序模型：单均匀分布前驱信号达到≥1/2成功率（优于经典1/e基准）；随信号越晚分布，成功率逼近1。对抗顺序模型：传统模型无法提供有效保证，但足够集中的前驱信号可恢复常数成功保证。

Conclusion: 异步时间信息是一种新颖且强大的在线决策辅助形式，仅凭到达时间即可显著提升算法性能。这一发现可推广至其他在线问题，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: In learning-augmented online algorithms, predictions are usually valued for what they say: a value estimate, a solution, or an algorithmic recommendation. This paper shows that predictions can also be valuable solely due to their arrival time. We study the fundamental secretary problem augmented with a stochastic precursor: a content-free signal that is guaranteed to arrive no later than the best item, but is otherwise stochastically timed. The signal does not carry any additional information; nevertheless, its timing alone changes the structure of optimal stopping. We characterize optimal policies in the random-order and adversarial-order models. In random order, a single uniformly timed precursor already gives success probability at least $\frac12$, improving on the classic $\frac1e$ benchmark. With increasingly late precursors, the success probability approaches $1$. In adversarial order, for which traditional models do not admit strong guarantees, sufficiently concentrated precursors recover constant success guarantees. Our results show that such novel forms of asynchronous temporal information are a distinct and powerful form of advice in online decision making and may also be effective for other problems.

</details>


### [62] [On the Parameterized Complexity of Min-Sum-Radii](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22804)
*Pankaj Kumar,Haiko Müller,Sebastian Ordyniak,Melanie Schmidt*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In the Min-Sum-Radii (MSR) clustering problem, we are given a finite set X of n points in a metric space. The objective is to find at most k clusters centered at a subset of these points such that every point of X is assigned to one of the clusters, minimizing the sum of the radii of the clusters. The problem is known to be NP-hard even on metrics induced by weighted planar graphs and metrics with constant doubling dimension, as shown by Gibson et al. (SWAT 2008). In this work, we investigate the parameterized complexity of MSR on metrics induced by undirected graphs. We distinguish between weighted graph metrics (with positive edge weights) and unweighted graph metrics (where all edges have unit weight).
  Weighted Graph Metrics: We show that MSR is W[1]-hard on metrics induced by weighted bipartite graphs, when parameterized by the combined parameter k (the number of clusters) and Delta (the cost of the clustering). We then investigate the structural parameterized complexity of the problem. Drexler et al. (arXiv:2310.02130) showed that the MSR problem admits an XP algorithm on metrics induced by weighted graphs when parameterized by treewidth, and asked whether this can be improved to fixed-parameter tractability. We first answer their question in the negative, and more strongly show that MSR stays W[1]-hard on metrics induced by undirected weighted bipartite graphs when parameterized by the vertex cover number plus k. We then turn our attention to parameters for dense graphs and show that MSR remains W[1]-hard when parameterized by k+Delta even on cliques and complete bipartite graphs. On the positive side, we employ the known XP algorithm parameterized by treewidth, to show that the MSR problem is FPT when parameterized by the parameter treewidth plus Delta.

</details>


### [63] [Optimal Testing of Reed-Muller Codes with an Online Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22813)
*Esty Kelman,Uri Meir,Kai Zhe Zheng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了半样本测试器模型，用于在在线擦除模型下测试Reed-Muller码和提升仿射不变码。通过先选取域子集再在子集内随机查询的混合策略，有效对抗对手的擦除攻击，实现了最优查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受在线擦除模型（Kalemaj等人2022/2023）下属性测试应用驱动，需解决对手可在每次查询后擦除最多t个输入点的 adversaries 问题，传统测试器的可预测查询模式可能被破坏。

Method: 定义了半样本测试器模型（介于纯样本测试器与标准测试器之间），通过先选择域的子集S，再在S内均匀随机查询的方式设计测试器，并对Reed-Muller码和提升仿射不变码进行了分析。

Result: 证明了半样本测试器在在线擦除模型下对Reed-Muller码有效，获得了最优查询复杂度，改进了Minzer和Zheng（SODA 2024）的先前工作，并首次将结果推广到提升仿射不变码。

Conclusion: 本文提出了半样本测试器模型，在在线擦除模型下实现了Reed-Muller码的最优查询复杂度，并首次给出了提升仿射不变码在半样本模型下的测试器。

Abstract: Motivated by applications to property testing in the online-erasure model of Kalemaj, Raskhodnikova, and Varma (ITCS 2022 and Theory of Computing 2023), we define and analyze {\em semi-sample-based testers} for Reed-Muller codes. The task in Reed-Muller testing is to determine whether an input function $f: \F^n \to \F$ belongs to the Reed-Muller code or is far from it, using as few point queries to $f$ as possible. Reed-Muller testing is a well-studied task with its roots in both the Property Testing and Probabilistically Checkable Proofs literature. The online-erasure model introduces a twist: after each query made, an adversary may erase up to $t$ points of the input function, potentially thwarting any test in which the queries follow a predictable pattern.
  Semi-sample-based testers are a hybrid between sample-based testers -- which can only make uniformly random queries to the input function -- and standard testers, which can choose their queries freely. They are designed with the online-erasure model in mind and operate by first choosing some subset $S$ of the domain and then making their queries uniformly at random inside of $S$. We describe semi-sample-based testers for the Reed-Muller code and give an optimal analysis of their soundness.
  Consequently, we show that semi-sample-based testers are indeed effective in the presence of online erasures, and thereby achieve optimal query complexity for testing the Reed-Muller code in the online-erasure model. This result improves upon prior work of Minzer and Zheng (SODA 2024). As an added bonus, we show that semi-sample-based testers also exist for the lifted affine-invariant codes of Guo, Kopparty, and Sudan (ITCS 2013), thereby providing the first known testers for these codes in the online-erasure model.

</details>

### [64] [Requirements Perception Gap across Stakeholders: A Comparative Survey of Aged Care Digital Health Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21495)
*Yuqing Xiao,John Grundy,Anuradha Madugalla,Elizabeth Manias*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法调查了老年人、护理人员和软件开发者三类利益相关者对老年护理数字健康软件的满意度与需求看法，发现存在显著的"需求差距"：开发者与用户群体在功能需求和非功能需求优先级上存在系统性分歧，研究成果可为共同设计流程和产品决策提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在弥合老年护理数字健康软件设计中普遍存在的"需求差距"问题。现实中软件开发者与终端用户（老年人及其护理人员）在软件功能需求与非功能需求优先级上存在显著分歧，需要系统性地探索和比较三类关键利益相关者的不同视角。

Method: 采用混合方法研究设计，结合定量问卷调查与定性开放式回答。样本量249人，包含103名老年人、41名护理人员（正式和非正式）、105名软件开发者。采用推理统计比较各组满意度水平，并通过主题分析处理定性开放式回答。

Result: 研究发现存在显著的"需求差距"：开发者倾向于优先考虑高级功能和功能性需求，明显高估用户对核心非功能需求（如易用性和响应性）的满意度；相比老年人和护理人员，开发者对现有功能特性持更挑剔态度；而老年人和护理人员则优先考虑简洁性和可靠性，而非功能密集度。通过整合定量与定性分析，研究明确了利益相关者需求的趋同点与分歧点。

Conclusion: 研究提出了利益相关者差距分析框架，可指导未来在老年护理数字健康领域的共同设计流程、产品决策制定以及隐私设计推荐。该研究具有重要的实践意义，弥合了开发者视角与用户实际需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We sought to explore and compare the perspectives of three key stakeholder groups: older adults, caregivers (formal health providers and informal caregivers), and digital health software developers on key functional and non-functional requirements. We conducted a survey, designed based on the findings from an existing systematic review, to gather and analyse data related to the three stakeholder groups' (dis)satisfaction with current aged care digital health software and their views on key future aged care software requirements. A mixed-methods survey approach integrated quantitative questionnaire data and qualitative open-ended responses from a total sample of 249, comprised of older adults (103), formal and informal caregivers (41), and software developers (105). Data analysis utilised a mixed methods approach, employing inferential statistics to compare group satisfaction levels and thematic analysis for qualitative open-ended responses. Our analysis reveals a significant "Requirements Gap". Software developers tend to prioritise advanced features and functional requirements, significantly overestimating user satisfaction with core NFRs such as ease of use and responsiveness. Conversely, developers were more critical of existing functional features compared to older adults and caregivers, who prioritised simplicity and reliability over feature density. By combining quantitative and qualitative analysis, we identified where stakeholder priorities align and where they diverge across functional and non-functional requirements in both the current designs they used and the future designs they desire. Our findings present a stakeholder gap analysis that can guide future co-design processes, near-term product decisions, and privacy-by-design recommendations in aged care digital health.

</details>

*Lukas Weidener,Marko Brkić,Mihailo Jovanović,Emre Ulgac,Aakaash Meduri*
