These contests serve as ideal proving grounds because they encode real-world complexity: ambiguous requirements, diverse data modalities, design trade-offs, and task-driven validation. Given only the data and target tasks, our system autonomously produces functional single-page VIS Apps with verified linked-view behavior, highly customized to domain experts' specified tasks and needs.

</details>


### [99] [FLUID: From Ephemeral IDs to Multimodal Semantic Codes for Industrial-Scale Livestreaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21832)
*Xinhang Yuan,Zexi Huang,Anjia Cao,Xudong Lu,Zikai Wang,Penghao Zhou,Chang Liu,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLUID框架，通过跨域多模态编码器生成离散层次编码(LUCID)替代传统物品ID，结合晚期融合设计解决直播推荐中物品ID冷启动问题。系统已在超10亿用户规模的生产环境中验证有效，冷启动和核心业务指标均显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播推荐场景存在独特挑战：直播间通常仅播出数十分钟，其物品ID在持久性冷启动状态下难以充分学习，传统ID-centric排序模型泛化能力失效。

Method: FLUID框架核心包括：(1)跨域多模态编码器，在短视频和直播数据上联合训练生成离散层次编码(LUCID)；(2)晚期融合的ID-free设计，将切片级和房间级LUCID作为独立token注入模型；(3)在线增量训练下的分阶段预热策略实现稳定训练。

Result: 在全球超过10亿用户的跨平台直播推荐系统中，FLUID取得显著在线效果：质量观看时长+0.55%、冷启动房间曝光+2.05%、活跃时长+0.05%。

Conclusion: FLUID成功将物品ID从生产级直播推荐排序模型中移除，结合跨域多模态编码器（LUCID）和晚期融合设计，通过增量训练中的分阶段预热稳定模型。该系统在超过10亿用户的全球平台上部署，显著提升冷启动房间曝光和质量观看时长等核心指标。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems rely heavily on ID-based collaborative filtering: each item is represented by a unique ID embedding that accumulates collaborative signals from user interactions. Livestreaming recommendation, however, faces a unique challenge in this paradigm: a live room typically broadcasts for only tens of minutes, so its item ID remains poorly learned in a persistent cold-start state and ID-centric ranking models fail to generalize. We present FLUID, the first framework to fully retire the candidate-side item ID from a production-scale livestreaming ranker. FLUID couples a cross-domain multimodal encoder, jointly trained on short videos and livestreams to produce discrete hierarchical codes (LUCID), with a late-fusion, ID-free design that injects slice-level and room-level LUCID as independent tokens, stabilized by a staged warmup under online incremental training. Deployed on our industrial livestreaming recommenders with a cross-platform combined user base of over one billion globally, FLUID delivers significant online gains of +0.55% Quality Watch Duration, +2.05% Cold-Start Room Views, and +0.05% Active Hours.

</details>


### [100] [Planning in the LLM Era: Building for Reliability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21902)
*Michael Katz,Harsha Kokel,Kavitha Srinivas,Shirin Sohrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，审视LLM时代智能体规划领域的发展趋势。作者追溯了从单阶段生成到混合搜索再到符号求解器生成的演进路径，论证当前规划研究正从"推理时依赖LLM"向"构建时生成符号求解器"范式转变，并系统分析了三类规划器生成方法的现状与局限，为未来可靠、高效的LLM规划研究指明方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体规划能力的可靠性与资源效率问题日益突出。早期方法（单阶段生成和混合搜索）存在本质缺陷：不健全且不完备，需要大量资源却无法在未见问题上获得更优解。这促使学界重新审视规划方法的根本架构。

Method: 论文采用分类综述方法，将现有LLM规划方法划分为三大类别进行系统梳理，分析各方法的核心局限：单阶段生成方法存在不健全/不完备性；混合方法资源消耗大；符号求解器生成方法具有可验证性和推理时高效性的优势。

Result: 论文明确了规划领域在LLM时代的新方向：将LLM应用于解决方案构建阶段，生成可验证的符号求解器，而非在推理时持续依赖LLM。这一转变不仅提高了可靠性与资源效率，还为生成可维护的规划器提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 本文论证了LLM时代规划领域正经历根本性范式转变——从在推理时依赖LLM逐步生成计划，转向在构建时生成可验证的符号求解器。三类规划器生成方法各有局限，作者呼吁学界推进更可靠、高效的LLM规划器生成研究。核心洞见：推理时对语言模型的依赖应最小化，以实现可维护的资源高效规划系统。

Abstract: Growing attention to intelligent agents has put a spotlight on one of their central capabilities: planning. Early attempts to leverage large language models (LLMs) for planning relied on single-shot plan generation, followed by hybrid approaches that coupled LLMs with limited external search. These methods, unsound and incomplete by their very nature, often require substantial resources without yielding better solutions on unseen problems. As the limitations of LLMs become clearer, recent work has shifted toward using them at solution construction time -- generating symbolic solvers for a family of problems that can be verified and then used efficiently at inference time. This trend reflects the growing need for agents that are both reliable and resource-efficient. It also offers a path towards generating maintainable planners with minimal dependence on language models at inference time. In this paper, we argue that this shift reflects a broader realignment of the planning field in the LLM era. We examine three major categories of planner-generation methods, discuss their current limitations, and outline research steps towards a more reliable and efficient LLM-based generation of planners.

</details>


### [101] [Format-Constraint Coupling in Knowledge Graph Construction from Statistical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21974)
*Jingxuan Qi,Zhiqiang Ye,Yuxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Identifies "format-constraint coupling" as a super-additive interaction between CSV serialization format and schema constraints that degrades KG fidelity on statistical data; surface-form anchoring via column-name references is the mechanism; proposes direct graph access as diagnostic tool; releases CSVFidelity-Bench benchmark.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schema extraction on statistical CSV data reduces knowledge graph fidelity through an interaction effect between serialization format and schema constraints ("format-constraint coupling"), with catastrophic mismatch when schema meets mismatched format.

Method: 2x2 factorial design across serialization format and schema constraints; bootstrap 95% CIs; controlled ablation on format-schema pairings, GraphRAG hosts, and LLM families; probing and token ablation experiments.

Result: Format-constraint coupling exceeds additive effects by up to +1.180 (2x2 factorial, 6 datasets); fact coverage drops below baseline on 4/6 datasets via entity inflation or extraction refusal; surface-form anchoring (column-name references) identified as mechanism; direct graph access exposes quality gaps up to +47.6pp (p<0.0001) while standard retrieval modes mask gaps (delta<=1pp).

Conclusion: Release of CSVFidelity-Bench (15 datasets, 1,892 Gold Standard facts) to support fidelity-aware evaluation; direct graph access reveals gaps up to +47.6pp whereas standard retrieval modes mask construction quality.

Abstract: An extraction schema should not reduce knowledge graph fidelity. On statistical CSV, however, it can. We study country-by-year time-series matrices, a common layout on open-data portals. In this setting, serialization format and schema constraints interact super-additively. Their joint effect exceeds the sum of independent effects by up to +1.180 (2x2 factorial, 6 datasets). Bootstrap 95% CIs are strictly positive on 4/6 datasets, with strongest evidence on wide Type-II matrices. More critically, a schema applied to a mismatched format can trigger catastrophic mismatch. Fact coverage falls below the unconstrained baseline on 4/6 datasets through entity inflation or extraction refusal. We call this observed pattern format-constraint coupling. Probing and token ablation support a surface-form anchoring explanation centred on column-name references. Controlled variants across format-schema pairings, GraphRAG hosts, and LLM families show the same direction within the measured scope; one LLM family shows only partial activation. The observation also has a diagnostic consequence. Three standard retrieval modes largely mask construction quality (delta <= 1pp), whereas direct graph access exposes gaps up to +47.6pp (p < 0.0001). To support fidelity-aware evaluation, we release CSVFidelity-Bench. It contains 15 datasets, 11 Type-II matrices, 4 Type-III tables, and 1,892 Gold Standard facts across 6 domains.

</details>


### [102] [Echo: Learning from Experience Data via User-Driven Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21984)
*Hande Dong,Xiaoyun Liang,Jiarui Yu,Jiayi Lin,Changqing Ai,Feng Liu,Wenjun Zhang,Rongbi Wei,Chaofan Zhu,Linjie Che,Feng Wu,Xin Shen,Dexu Kong,Xiaotian Wang,Qiuyuan Chen,Bingxu An,Yueting Lei,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Echo是一个将原始交互经验转化为可训练知识的通用框架，通过系统化利用用户修正行为作为环境反馈信号，在生产代码补全场景中将接受率提升10个百分点，证明了从"经验数据"持续学习的可行性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态人类数据面临可扩展性差、成本高、且受限于创建者知识边界的固有局限。传统方法依赖人工标注数据，效率低下且难以持续优化。

Method: Echo框架将原始交互日志转化为可学习知识，系统性地利用用户修正序列作为环境反馈信号。用户通过将不完美的智能体输出修正为验证解的过程，本质上是对粗糙尝试的蒸馏，形成高质量训练信号。

Result: 在生产级代码补全环境中验证，智能体接受率从25.7%显著提升至35.7%，证明该方法能有效打破静态性能天花板。

Conclusion: Echo框架在生产代码补全环境中验证有效，将接受率从25.7%提升至35.7%，打破了静态性能上限，证明了通过用户修正信号持续对齐AI智能体的可行性。

Abstract: Static "human data" faces inherent limitations: it is expensive to scale and bounded by the knowledge of its creators. Continuous learning from "experience data" - interactions between agents and their environments - promises to transcend these barriers. Today, the widespread deployment of AI agents grants us low-cost access to massive streams of such real-world experience. However, raw interaction logs are inherently noisy, filled with trial-and-error and low information density, rendering them inefficient for direct model training.
  We introduce Echo, a generalized framework designed to operationalize the transition from raw experience to learnable knowledge, effectively "echoing" environmental feedback back into the training loop for model optimization. In today's agent ecosystem, user refinement serves as a primary source of such feedback: driven by responsibility for the outcome, users rigorously transform flawed agent proposals into verified solutions. These user-driven refinement sequences inherently distill agents' crude attempts into high-quality training signals. Echo systematically harvests these signals to continuously align the agent with real-world needs. Large-scale validation in a production code completion environment confirms that Echo effectively harnesses this pipeline, breaking the static performance ceiling by increasing the acceptance rate from 25.7% to 35.7%.

</details>


### [103] [Active Evidence-Seeking and Diagnostic Reasoning in Large Language Models for Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22047)
*Chen Zhan,Xihe Qiu,Xiaoyu Tan,Xibing Zhuang,Gengchen Ma,Yue Zhang,Shuo Li,Peifeng Liu,Xiaoxiao Ge,Liang Liu,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个OSCE风格的标准化患者模拟器基准测试，发现当前大语言模型在交互式诊断推理中存在显著的性能下降（准确率下降12.75%，证据质量下降24.36%），提示静态医学考试高估了模型在真实临床场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在静态医学考试中表现良好，但临床诊断通常需要迭代式的不确定性证据收集。现有交互式评估方法不足，需要建立更贴近临床实际的标准化测试协议。

Method: 采用OSCE（客观结构化临床考试）风格的标准化患者模拟器，对468个病例和15个模型进行受控的可复现基准测试，评估多轮证据收集能力。

Result: 多轮证据搜索相比全上下文评估，诊断准确率下降12.75%，支持证据质量下降24.36%；错误分析显示这与过早诊断闭合和低效提问相关。

Conclusion: 静态全上下文基准测试可能高估了交互式证据搜索场景中的实际表现，提示需要补充交互式评估方法以支持更安全的临床决策支持系统开发。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on static medical examinations, yet clinical diagnosis often requires iterative evidence gathering under uncertainty. Building on prior interactive evaluation efforts, we introduce an OSCE-inspired standardized patient simulator and a controlled, reproducible benchmark for active diagnostic inquiry. Across 468 cases and 15 models in our protocol, we observe that multi-turn evidence seeking reduces diagnostic accuracy by 12.75% and lowers supporting-evidence quality by 24.36% relative to full-context evaluation; error analyses associate these drops with premature diagnostic closure and inefficient questioning. Together, these results suggest that static full-context benchmarks may overestimate performance in interactive evidence-seeking settings, motivating complementary interactive assessment for safer clinical decision support.

</details>


### [104] [Enhancing Visual Token Representations for Video Large Language Models via Training-Free Spatial-Temporal Pooling and Gridding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22078)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Hanqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ST-GridPool，一种无需训练的视觉token增强方法，通过金字塔时间网格(PTG)捕获多粒度时空交互，并通过基于范数的空间池化(NSP)保留高信息量视觉区域，实现Video LLMs的高效即插即用性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing visual token compression methods in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), such as those used in LLaVA family, rely on simplistic pooling or interpolation techniques that overlook the intricate dynamics of visual tokens, creating a gap between efficient compression and preservation of spatiotemporal interactions.

Method: ST-GridPool integrates two key components: (1) Pyramid Temporal Gridding (PTG) captures multi-grained spatiotemporal interactions through hierarchical temporal gridding, and (2) Norm-based Spatial Pooling (NSP) preserves high-information visual regions by leveraging the correlation between token norms and semantic richness. The method requires no retraining.

Result: Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that ST-GridPool consistently enhances the performance of Video LLMs without requiring costly retraining.

Conclusion: ST-GridPool provides an efficient, training-free, and plug-and-play solution for enhancing visual token representations in Video LLMs, demonstrating consistent performance improvements across various benchmarks.

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced video understanding tasks, yet challenges remain in efficiently compressing visual tokens while preserving spatiotemporal interactions. Existing methods, such as LLaVA family, utilize simplistic pooling or interpolation techniques that overlook the intricate dynamics of visual tokens. To bridge this gap, we propose ST-GridPool, a novel training-free visual token enhancement method designed specifically for Video LLMs. Our approach integrates Pyramid Temporal Gridding (PTG), which captures multi-grained spatiotemporal interactions through hierarchical temporal gridding, and Norm-based Spatial Pooling (NSP), which preserves high-information visual regions by leveraging the correlation between token norms and semantic richness. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that ST-GridPool consistently enhances performance of Video LLMs without requiring costly retraining. Our method offers an efficient and plug-and-play solution for improving visual token representations. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-GridPool.

</details>


### [105] [A Camera-Cooperative ISAC Framework for Multimodal Non-Cooperative UAVs Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22090)
*Wenfeng Wu,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Camera-Cooperative ISAC (CC-ISAC)框架，利用摄像头进行粗粒度空域监测，ISAC进行细粒度高精度感知，通过V2EDA和MMFE两个关键模块实现高效无人机波束控制与跟踪。实验表明该框架可显著降低开销并缓解资源竞争问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISAC系统中，单模态感知存在固有局限，且感知与通信资源共享导致竞争问题，难以有效检测非合作无人机。

Method: 1) Vision-to-Echo Data Alignment (V2EDA)模型：通过跨注意力机制对齐视觉域和回波域特征；2) Multimodal Fusion-Based Estimation (MMFE)模型：融合历史多模态数据与当前观测进行鲁棒状态估计。实验基于DeepSense 6G数据集进行评估。

Result: 该框架实现了平均71%的波束控制开销降低，以及1.69-11.15%的跟踪开销降低，同时保持高角度估计精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的CC-ISAC框架有效缓解了感知与通信之间的资源竞争，实现了可靠的无人机监控，同时为额外的通信任务释放了显著的系统资源，代表了ISAC系统设计的实用进展。

Abstract: The detection of non-cooperative unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) presents significant challenges for Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems due to the inherent limitations of single-modal perception and the competition for shared communication and sensing resources. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Camera-Cooperative ISAC (CC-ISAC) framework that employs multimodal sensing to enable efficient UAV beam steering and tracking. The proposed framework employs cameras for coarse-grained airspace monitoring and utilizes ISAC for fine-grained, high-precision sensing, forming a complementary perception loop that enhances both sensing accuracy and resource efficiency. Within this framework, two key modules are developed: (1) a Vision-to-Echo Data Alignment (V2EDA) model that aligns visual and echo-domain features through cross-attention mechanisms, and (2) a Multimodal Fusion-Based Estimation (MMFE) model that integrates historical multimodal data with current observations for robust state estimation. Extensive evaluations conducted on the DeepSense 6G dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average reduction of 71% in beam steering overhead and 1.69-11.15% in tracking overhead while maintaining high angular estimation accuracy. The CC-ISAC framework effectively mitigates resource contention between sensing and communication, enabling reliable UAV surveillance while freeing substantial system resources for additional communication tasks, thereby representing a practical advancement in ISAC system design.

</details>


### [106] [Knowledge Graph Re-engineering Along the Ontological Continuum (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22093)
*Enrico Daga,Valentina Tamma,Terry Payne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"本体论连续体"作为知识图谱领域缺失的概念化框架，通过语义/语用与属性/功能两个正交维度描述知识图谱建模实践的全谱系，采用经验主义方法从实际工程实践中提取理论，并以形式概念分析形式化该结构，旨在为神经符号AI和生成式AI时代的知识图谱集成与自动化提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱建模实践多样（从轻量级词汇到丰富公理化本体），导致集成和复用成本高、脆弱性强。在神经符号AI中，弥合神经与符号组件的能力取决于重新设计知识图谱；生成式AI虽提供自动化能力，但缺乏对知识图谱空间的原理性理解使其概念上无根基。

Method: 采用经验主义方法论，通过观察真实世界的知识图谱工程实践来构建理论；利用形式概念分析（FCA）将连续体结构形式化；通过溯源知识案例研究展示单一关注点如何在连续体上呈现不同表现形式。

Result: 定义了由"语义/语用"和"属性/功能"两个正交维度构成的本体论连续体表征框架；展示了溯源知识这一单一关注点如何在连续体的不同位置呈现不同形态。

Conclusion: 提出了本体论连续体的概念框架，由语义/语用和属性/功能两个正交维度构成，可用于描述、比较、导航和转换知识图谱。该研究邀请学界将本体论连续体发展为共享研究议程，并列出了五个开放研究挑战。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become the primary vehicle for data integration and are critical to the success of modern AI, but the diversity of KG modelling practices, from lightweight vocabularies to richly axiomatised ontologies, makes integration and reuse expensive and brittle. This challenge is particularly acute in neuro-symbolic AI, where bridging neural and symbolic components depends on the ability to reengineer KGs to fit new requirements; GenAI now offers unprecedented automation capability, but without a principled understanding of the KG space, such automation remains conceptually ungrounded. We introduce the ontological continuum as that missing conceptualisation, a theoretical construct a theoretical construct whose characterisation framework is defined by two orthogonal distinctions: semantics vs pragmatics, and properties vs affordances; together these define a vocabulary to describe, compare, navigate, and transform KGs across the full range of modelling practices. The methodological stance is empirical: rather than prescribing how KGs should be modelled, the continuum aims to define a theory of the existent, derived from observation of real-world KG engineering practices and whose structure can be made formally explicit, for example, through Formal Concept Analysis (FCA). We ground the vision through a case study on provenance knowledge, showing how a single concern manifests differently across the continuum. We articulate five open research challenges and invite the community to develop the ontological continuum as a shared research agenda.

</details>


### [107] [MPDocBench-Parse: Benchmarking Practical Multi-page Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22100)
*Bangbang Zhou,Hangdi Xing,Yifan Chen,Jianjun Xu,Qi Zheng,Feiyu Gao,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MPDocBench-Parse是一个面向真实场景的多页文档解析基准，包含433份文档（3,246页），覆盖15种文档类型，支持文档级端到端评估和细粒度的内容保真度与逻辑结构评估，为推进文档解析向更现实场景发展提供统一基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准存在不足：仅关注特定任务或单页文本场景，缺乏实际多页解析能力；对语义连续性、层级结构恢复和视觉内容保存的细粒度评估不足

Method: 构建包含433份手动标注文档（3,246页）的大规模基准，覆盖15种文档类型（英汉双语），支持文档级端到端评估；设计全面的内容保真度和逻辑结构评估协议

Result: 现有模型在基础文本提取上表现良好，但在语义连续性整合、视觉内容解析和层级结构恢复方面存在明显不足

Conclusion: MPDocBench-Parse为推进文档解析向更现实的场景发展提供了统一基础，实验表明现有模型在文本提取上表现良好，但在语义连续性整合、视觉内容解析和层次结构恢复方面仍有明显局限。

Abstract: Document parsing converts visually rich documents into machine-readable structured representations, forming a crucial foundation for information systems. Although many benchmarks have been proposed for document parsing, they remain inadequate for realistic scenarios. Existing benchmarks either focus on specific tasks or assess only single-page, text-centric settings, making them insufficient for practical multi-page parsing. Moreover, they lack fine-grained evaluation of semantic continuity, hierarchical structure recovery, and visual content preservation. To address these gaps, we propose MPDocBench-Parse, a benchmark for multi-page document parsing in real-world applications. It contains 433 manually annotated documents with 3,246 pages, covering 15 document types in English and Chinese, with diverse layout styles, and supports document-level end-to-end evaluation. We further design a comprehensive protocol for content fidelity and logical structure, covering text, table, and formula recognition, truncated text and table merging, figure extraction, reading order, and heading hierarchy recovery. Experiments show that, while existing models perform well on basic text extraction, they still suffer clear limitations in semantic continuity integration, visual content parsing, and hierarchical structure recovery. MPDocBench-Parse provides a unified foundation for advancing document parsing toward more realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [108] [ExComm: Exploration-Stage Communication for Error-Resilient Agentic Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22102)
*Woomin Song,Beomjun Kim,Daewon Choi,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Saket Dingliwal,Jinwoo Shin,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExComm通信协议，通过周期性审计检测跨智能体事实冲突，调用工具验证循环解决冲突并以软更新方式整合反馈，同时引入轨迹多样化模块防止通信导致的探索多样性崩塌。实验表明该方法在多基准测试中一致超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体测试时扩展中的错误传播问题：中间步骤产生的事实错误或无效推论会持续存在于智能体信念状态中，污染后续推理。现有方法控制能力有限，过度依赖智能体自我错误检测、轨迹选择或事后优化。

Method: ExComm采用通信协议框架，核心机制包括：(1)周期性审计智能体信念状态以检测跨智能体事实冲突；(2)通过专用工具验证循环解决冲突；(3)软信念更新策略（追加验证反馈而非覆盖）；(4)轨迹多样化模块引导冗余轨迹转向正交策略。

Result: 在AIME 2024、AIME 2025、GAIA三个基准上使用Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite和Qwen3.5-4B模型测试，相比最佳基线分别提升5.7%和5.0%；具备更优的错误恢复能力、良好的扩展行为、强于基线的轨迹多样性，以及最佳的性能-成本权衡。

Conclusion: ExComm通过冲突检测与解决机制有效解决长时程推理中的错误传播问题，轨迹多样化模块维持了探索多样性，在多个基准测试中实现最佳性能-成本权衡。

Abstract: A common failure mode in long-horizon agentic test-time scaling is error propagation, where factual errors or invalid deductions introduced at intermediate steps persist in the agent's belief state and contaminate later reasoning. Existing test-time scaling methods provide limited control over this process, as they often rely on agents to detect their own mistakes, select among flawed trajectories, or refine solutions only after errors have already shaped the reasoning path. We propose ExComm, a communication protocol for exploration-stage agentic test-time scaling. ExComm is motivated by the empirical observation that the majority of intermediate errors in parallel agentic reasoning produce detectable cross-agent factual conflicts. Leveraging the iterative structure of agentic workflows, ExComm periodically audits agent belief states to detect such conflicts, resolves them through a dedicated tool-based verification loop, and returns concise, targeted feedback to the involved agents. Corrections are incorporated through soft belief updates, which append verified feedback rather than overwriting existing beliefs. Furthermore, to prevent collapsing trajectory diversity due to communication, ExComm further introduces a trajectory diversification module that redirects redundant trajectories toward orthogonal strategies. Experiments on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and GAIA with Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite and Qwen3.5-4B show that ExComm consistently outperforms strong test-time scaling baselines, achieving average performance gains of 5.7% and 5.0% over the best-performing baselines, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate improved error recovery, favorable scaling behavior, stronger diversity than adapted communication baselines, and the best performance-cost trade-off among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [109] [ArborKV: Structure-Aware KV Cache Management for Scaling Tree-based LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22106)
*Yeqiu Chen,Ziyan Liu,Zhenxin Huang,Runquan Gui,Hong Wang,Lei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对Tree-of-Thoughts推理中KV缓存内存瓶颈问题，提出ArborKV结构感知驱逐框架，通过价值估计和树感知策略实现高效内存管理，在保持准确率的同时将峰值KV内存降低约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存成为ToT推理中的内存瓶颈：维护前沿partial轨迹的KV状态导致内存快速耗尽，限制了吞吐量和在固定硬件预算下的搜索深度与宽度。

Method: ArborKV采用结构感知的驱逐框架，结合轻量级价值估计器和树感知分配策略，执行纯token提取式驱逐（token-extractive eviction）并支持懒重新水合（lazy rehydration）以实现回溯访问。

Result: ArborKV实现了最高约4倍的峰值KV内存 reduction，同时保持接近完整保留的准确性。

Conclusion: ArborKV enables larger搜索配置（深度和宽度）在固定硬件 budgets下运行，否则会因内存不足而无法执行。

Abstract: Recent progress in LLM reasoning has increasingly shifted from single-pass generation to explicit search over intermediate reasoning states. Tree-of-Thoughts (ToT) organizes inference to tree-structured search with branching and backtracking, but it substantially amplifies the Key--Value (KV) cache: retaining KV states for a frontier of partial trajectories quickly becomes a memory bottleneck that limits throughput and constrains search depth and width under fixed hardware budgets. We address this challenge by observing that KV reuse in ToT-style inference is governed by search dynamics: near-term decoding depends primarily on the active branch and its ancestors, whereas inactive subtrees have low short-term reuse probability yet must remain recoverable for backtracking. Motivated by this, we propose ArborKV, a structure-aware eviction framework that couples a lightweight value estimator with a tree-aware allocation policy, and performs purely token-extractive eviction with lazy rehydration to support revisits. Experiments on ToT-style reasoning benchmarks show that ArborKV achieves up to ~4x peak KV-memory reduction while preserving near-full-retention accuracy, enabling larger search configurations under fixed device budgets that would otherwise run out of memory.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient Agentic Reasoning Through Self-Regulated Simulative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22138)
*Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou,Lara Sá Neves,Varad Pimpalkhute,Taylor W. Killian,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SR²AM 架构，将 LLM Agent 的决策过程分解为自调节（System III）、模拟推理（System II）和反应式执行（System I）三个系统，实现高效规划。在多个基准上以小得多的模型规模和 Token 消耗达到与超大模型相当的性能，证明显式规划控制是提升 Agent 效率的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于端到端反应式策略的 Agent 在规划时缺乏显式控制，导致推理长度大幅增加、Token 使用低效、准确率提升不可靠。

Method: 将 Simulative Reasoning (System II) 和 Self-Regulation (System III) 实现为 LLM 思维链内的独立阶段，使用 LLM 本身作为世界模型；通过监督学习微调后接强化学习（RL）进行训练。

Result: v0.1-8B 和 v1.0-30B 在数学、科学、表格分析和网页信息检索任务上，Pass@1 可比肩参数量为其 10-60 倍的模型；v1.0-30B 使用的推理 Token 比同类 Agentic LLM 少 25.8-95.3%。RL 训练使规划深度增加 22.8%，而规划频率仅增加 2.0%。

Conclusion: SR²AM 通过将规划能力构建为 LLM 思维链中的明确阶段，在多个基准测试上实现 SOTA 效率，验证了三系统分解架构的有效性，并指出自调节原则可推广至 Agent 的自主学习与适应。

Abstract: How should an agent decide when and how to plan? A dominant approach builds agents as reactive policies with adaptive computation (e.g., chain-of-thought), trained end-to-end expecting planning to emerge implicitly. Without control over the presence, structure, or horizon of planning, these systems dramatically increase reasoning length, yielding inefficient token use without reliable accuracy gains. We argue efficient agentic reasoning benefits from decomposing decision-making into three systems: simulative reasoning (System II) grounding deliberation in future-state prediction via a world model; self-regulation (System III) deciding when and how deeply to plan via a learned configurator; and reactive execution (System I) handling fine-grained action. Simulative reasoning provides unified planning across diverse tasks without per-domain engineering, while self-regulation ensures the planner is invoked only when needed. To test this, we develop SR$^2$AM (Self-Regulated Simulative Reasoning Agentic LLM), realizing both as distinct stages within an LLM's chain-of-thought, with the LLM as world model. We explore two instantiations: recording decisions from a prompted multi-module system (v0.1) and reconstructing structured plans from traces of pretrained reasoning LLMs (v1.0), trained via supervised then reinforcement learning (RL). Across math, science, tabular analysis, and web information seeking, v0.1-8B and v1.0-30B achieve Pass@1 competitive with 120-355B and 685B-1T parameter systems respectively, while v1.0-30B uses 25.8-95.3% fewer reasoning tokens than comparable agentic LLMs. RL increases average planning horizon by 22.8% while planning frequency grows only 2.0%, showing it learns to plan further ahead rather than more often. More broadly, learned self-regulation instantiates a principle we expect to extend beyond planning to how agents govern their own learning and adaptation.

</details>


### [111] [Ratchet: A Minimal Hygiene Recipe for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22148)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ratchet通过让冻结的LLM自主管理技能生命周期（编写、检索、整理、退休），解决了现有自演化技能库的核心瓶颈——不是技能编写而是生命周期管理。在MBPP+ hard-100上从0.258提升至0.584峰值0.658，并在SWE-bench Verified上成功迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现LLM编写的技能相比无技能基线无提升(+0.0pp)，而人类策展技能提升+16.2pp，证明瓶颈不在于技能编写而在于技能的全生命周期管理。现有系统缺少对技能退休和整理的机制。

Method: 单智能体循环框架，集成四种候选卫生机制：结果驱动退休、有界活跃技能上限、元技能编写指导、模式规范化。在MBPP+ hard-100上使用Claude Opus 4.7进行100轮3种子实验。

Result: MBPP+ hard-100: 基线0.258±0.047提升至滚动均值0.584（峰值0.658±0.042），增益+0.328±0.018，无技能对照仅+0.002±0.005。SWE-bench Verified: 20轮峰值提升+0.22。消融实验表明最小可行方案比设计更精简：退休机制和元技能编写先验是必需组件，而显式去重被元技能本身所替代。

Conclusion: 技能生命周期管理是自演化系统的关键；退休机制和元技能指导是核心组件；非发散性命题证明有界上限与退休阈值共同防止性能降至无技能基线以下。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries, pioneered by Voyager, let frozen LLM agents accumulate reusable knowledge without weight updates, yet recent evaluation shows that LLM-authored skills deliver $+0.0$pp over no-skill baselines while human-curated ones deliver $+16.2$pp: the bottleneck is not skill authoring but lifecycle management. We introduce \textbf{Ratchet}, a single-agent loop in which a frozen LLM writes, retrieves, curates, and retires its own natural-language skills. Ratchet integrates four candidate hygiene mechanisms: outcome-driven retirement, a bounded active-cap, meta-skill authoring guidance, and pattern canonicalisation. On MBPP+ hard-100 with Claude Opus 4.7, Ratchet lifts held-out pass@1 from a $0.258 \pm 0.047$ baseline to a late-window rolling mean of $0.584$ (peak $0.658 \pm 0.042$) across 100 rounds and 3 seeds, a $+0.328 \pm 0.018$ rolling-mean gain where the no-skill control drifts at $+0.002 \pm 0.005$; the same recipe transfers to an agentic solver on SWE-bench Verified ($+0.22$ peak lift over 20 rounds). Eight ablations (A1--A8) reveal that the minimal working recipe is smaller than our design suggests: retirement and the meta-skill authoring prior are load-bearing, while explicit deduplication (canonicalisation, cover-guard) is subsumed by the meta-skill itself. A non-divergence proposition shows that bounded cap and retirement threshold together prevent expected performance from drifting below the no-skills floor.

</details>


### [112] [ST-SimDiff: Balancing Spatiotemporal Similarity and Difference for Efficient Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22158)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Chaoqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-SimDiff框架，通过结合相似性和差异性分析来高效处理长视频中的大量视觉token。框架利用时空图建模token关联，并采用双选择策略：基于相似性的社区检测压缩静态信息，基于时序差异检测保留关键动态变化 token，在显著降低计算成本的同时超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过剪枝或合并token来减少冗余，但存在两个主要缺陷：1）忽视视频内容中的变化和转折点这一关键维度；2）缺乏对时空关系的协同建模能力。这导致压缩后的视频表示无法有效保留关键事件信息。

Method: ST-SimDiff采用训练无关框架，包含两个核心组件：1）构建时空图统一建模视觉token的复杂关联；2）并行双选择策略——相似性选择通过社区检测保留代表性token以压缩静态信息，时序差异选择通过检测内容变化点保留捕捉动态变化的token。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在保持优异性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的"相似性识别冗余，差异性捕捉关键事件"新视角为视频token压缩提供了有效范式，ST-SimDiff在效率和效果上均展现优势。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face significant computational overhead when processing long videos due to the massive number of visual tokens required. To improve efficiency, existing methods primarily reduce redundancy by pruning or merging tokens based on importance or similarity. However, these approaches largely overlook a critical dimension of video content, i.e., changes and turning points, and they lack a collaborative model for spatio-temporal relationships. To address this, we propose a new perspective: similarity is for identifying redundancy, while difference is for capturing key events. Based on this, we designed a training-free framework named ST-SimDiff. We first construct a spatio-temporal graph from the visual tokens to uniformly model their complex associations. Subsequently, we employ a parallel dual-selection strategy: 1) similarity-based selection uses community detection to retain representative tokens, compressing static information; 2) temporal difference-based selection precisely locates content-changing points to preserve tokens that capture key dynamic shifts. This allows it to preserve both static and dynamic content with a minimal number of tokens. Extensive experiments show our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches while substantially reducing computational costs. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-SimDiff.

</details>


### [113] [Adapting the Interface, Not the Model: Runtime Harness Adaptation for Deterministic LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22166)
*Tianshi Xu,Huifeng Wen,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Life-Harness，一种无需修改模型权重即可提升冻结 LLM 智能体性能的运行时 harness。它通过学习训练轨迹中的失败模式，将交互问题转化为跨多个维度（环境契约、程序技能、动作实现、轨迹调控）的可复用干预措施。在 7 个环境、18 个模型、126 个设置中实现了 88.5% 的平均相对提升，证明运行时接口适配是模型中心化训练的有效补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体不仅由语言模型塑造，还由管理观察、工具使用、动作执行、反馈解释和轨迹控制的运行时 harness 决定。现有智能体适配方法主要更新模型参数，但许多失败源于模型-环境界面的不匹配，而非模型本身的能力不足。

Method: 提出 Life-Harness，一个生命周期感知的运行时 harness。它从训练轨迹中学习，将反复出现的交互失败转化为跨环境契约、程序性技能、动作实现和轨迹调节的可复用干预措施，且在留出评估时保持固定。

Result: 在 7 个来自 τ-bench、τ²-bench 和 AgentBench 的确定性环境中，Life-Harness 在 18 个模型骨架的 126 个模型-环境设置中改进了 116 个，平均相对改进达 88.5%。仅用 Qwen3-4B-Instruct 轨迹演化的 harness 可迁移到其他 17 个模型，表明 Life-Harness 捕获的是可复用的环境端结构而非模型特定行为。

Conclusion: 运行时接口适配是模型中心化智能体训练方法的互补替代方案。该研究证明无需修改模型权重或评估环境，仅通过改进运行时 harness 就能显著提升 LLM 智能体的性能。

Abstract: LLM agents are shaped not only by their language models, but also by the runtime harness that mediates observation, tool use, action execution, feedback interpretation, and trajectory control. While existing agent adaptation methods mainly update model parameters, many failures in deterministic, rule-governed domains stem from mismatches at the model--environment interface. We propose Life-Harness, a lifecycle-aware runtime harness that improves frozen LLM agents without changing model weights or evaluation environments. Life-Harness evolves from training trajectories by converting recurring interaction failures into reusable interventions across environment contracts, procedural skills, action realization, and trajectory regulation, and remains fixed during held-out evaluation. On seven deterministic environments from $τ$-bench, $τ^2$-bench, and AgentBench, Life-Harness improves 116 out of 126 model--environment settings across 18 model backbones, with an average relative improvement of 88.5%. Harnesses evolved only from Qwen3-4B-Instruct trajectories transfer to 17 other models, showing that Life-Harness captures reusable environment-side structure rather than model-specific behavior. These results position runtime interface adaptation as a complementary alternative to model-centric agent training. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [114] [LLM-Metrics: Measuring Research Impact Through Large Language Model Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22176)
*Si Shen,Wenhua Zhao,Danhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-Metrics，利用大语言模型的参数记忆来评估研究影响力。该方法基于假设：高影响力论文获得更多曝光进入LLM训练数据，模型因此形成更强的记忆。通过549篇CS论文、17个LLM（0.5B-72B参数）的多选探针测试，总体Spearman相关系数ρ=0.1495（p=0.0004），表明LLM记忆与引用量存在显著正相关，提供了一种实时、跨学科、独立于引用的研究评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引用计数存在时间滞后、学科偏差和马太效应等固有局限，需要新的研究影响力评估方法。

Method: 设计四类多选探针（标题、作者、方法、 venue识别），对549篇2023-2024年CS论文在17个LLM（0.5B-72B参数，6个厂商）上进行测试。

Result: 17个模型中15个呈正相关，9个显著（p<0.05）；总体Spearman ρ=0.1495（p=0.0004）；2024论文相关性更强（ρ=0.1880）；作者识别探针区分力最强；模型规模与预测能力呈非单调关系，Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct（3B参数）表现优于多数更大模型（ρ=0.1829）。

Conclusion: LLM-Metrics提供了一种实时、跨学科、独立于引用的研究影响力评估新范式。小模型可能通过选择性记忆机制充当有效信息过滤器。

Abstract: Citation counts remain the dominant metric for assessing research impact, yet they suffer from well-documented limitations: temporal lag, disciplinary bias, and Matthew effects. Here we propose LLM-Metrics, a research-impact assessment metric derived from the parametric memory of large language models (LLMs). The central hypothesis is that high-impact papers receive greater exposure in the academic community, that this exposure enters LLM training data in textual form, and that models consequently form stronger parametric memory of these papers. We designed four types of multiple-choice probes, covering title recognition, author recognition, method recognition, and venue recognition, and evaluated 549 computer science papers published in 2023-2024 across 17 LLMs spanning 0.5B to 72B parameters from six vendors. Of the 17 models, 15 produced positive predictions, 9 of which were significant at p less than 0.05, with an overall Spearman correlation of rho = 0.1495 and p = 0.0004 against citation counts. Three additional findings support the proposed mechanism. First, the predictive signal was stronger for 2024 papers, rho = 0.1880, whose citation counts were near zero at model-training time, reducing the plausibility of a simple reverse-causality explanation. Second, author-recognition probes showed the strongest discriminative power, consistent with an exposure-driven memory mechanism. Third, model scale and predictive power were non-monotonic: a 3B-parameter model, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, with rho = 0.1829, outperformed most larger models, supporting a selective-memory hypothesis in which the limited capacity of smaller models can serve as an effective information filter. LLM-Metrics offers a real-time, cross-disciplinary, citation-independent paradigm for research assessment.

</details>


### [115] [Skill Weaving: Efficient LLM Improvement via Modular Skillpacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22205)
*Zhuo Li,Guodong Du,Zesheng Shi,Weiyang Guo,Weijun Yao,Yuan Zhou,Jiabo Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillWeave是一个模块化改进框架，通过轻量级领域特定增量模块（skillpacks）对通用模型进行专业化，并使用SkillZip压缩技术实现高效部署，使9B模型性能超越32B单片模型，同时实现4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型需要在多样化领域实现专业化，但现有方法难以在多领域能力与严格的内存和推理约束之间取得平衡。

Method: SkillWeave将通用模型的完整能力划分为skillpacks（轻量级领域特定增量模块），重组并精炼模型内部知识。集成SkillZip将skillpacks压缩为紧凑的推理就绪格式，实现低延迟执行。

Result: 在多任务和智能体基准测试中，9B SkillWeave模型超越多个基线，甚至超越32B单片LLM，同时实现最高4倍加速。

Conclusion: 模块化方法可在固定内存预算下实现强大的多领域性能，SkillZip压缩技术使高效部署成为可能，为资源受限场景下的LLM专业化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly require specialization across diverse domains, yet existing approaches struggle to balance multi-domain capacities with strict memory and inference constraints. In this work, we introduce SkillWeave, a modular improvement framework that enables LLMs to specialize under fixed memory budgets. SkillWeave partitions full capabilities of a general-purpose model into skillpacks -- lightweight, domain-specific delta modules -- that reorganize and refine the model's internal knowledge. For efficient deployment, SkillWeave integrates SkillZip to compress skillpacks into compact and inference-ready format, enabling strong multi-domain performance with low-latency execution. On multi-task and agentic benchmarks, a 9B SkillWeave model outperforms several baselines and even surpasses a 32B monolithic LLM, while achieving up to 4x speedup.

</details>


### [116] [CLORE: Content-Level Optimization for Reasoning Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22211)
*Yuyang Wu,Qiyao Xue,Guanxing Lu,Weichen Liu,Zihan Wang,Manling Li,Olexandr Isayev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLORE通过在正确轨迹上执行内容级编辑（删除重复、无关和冗余推理内容），结合参考无关的DPO目标优化，在保持准确率的同时显著提升推理效率，解决了现有方法仅依赖长度预算而忽视中间推理内容监督的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习后训练虽提升了LLM推理能力，但常产生不必要的长、重复或语义不透明的推理痕迹。现有高效推理方法主要通过显式预算或长度感知奖励调节响应长度，对中间推理内容的监督不足。

Method: 提出CLORE框架，使用外部增强模型在解题完成后删除重复段落、难以理解或任务无关的内容及多余推理，保留最终答案。将原始-编辑后的配对与标准策略梯度训练结合，用辅助参考无关的DPO目标优化。通过限制增强仅在正确轨迹上执行并执行局部删除，保持编辑后轨迹接近策略分布，缓解离策略不匹配问题。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上，于五个数学推理基准的实验表明CLORE提升了准确率-效率权衡，并与GRPO、DAPO、Training Efficient及ThinkPrune兼容。内容分析显示CLORE减少了重复推理、难以理解的内容和答案后探索。

Conclusion: 内容级监督可作为长度级控制方法的补充方向，为改进LLM推理效率提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training has improved the reasoning ability of large language models, but often produces unnecessarily long, repetitive, or semantically opaque reasoning traces. Existing efficient reasoning methods mainly regulate response length through explicit budgets or length-aware rewards, leaving intermediate reasoning content weakly supervised. We propose CLORE, a content-level optimization framework that improves reasoning efficiency by editing correct on-policy rollouts. CLORE uses an external augmentation model to delete repetitive segments, illegible or task-irrelevant content, and superfluous reasoning after the solution is established, while preserving the final answer. The resulting augmented--original pairs are optimized with an auxiliary reference-free DPO objective alongside standard policy-gradient training. By restricting augmentation to correct trajectories and performing local deletion, CLORE keeps edited rollouts close to the policy distribution and mitigates off-policy mismatch. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that CLORE improves the accuracy--efficiency trade-off and remains compatible with GRPO, DAPO, Training Efficient, and ThinkPrune. Content-level analyses further show that CLORE reduces repetitive reasoning, illegible content, and post-answer exploration, supporting content-level supervision as a complementary direction to length-level control.

</details>


### [117] [SGR-Bench: Benchmarking Search Agents on State-Gated Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22219)
*Ningyuan Li,Haiyang Shen,Mugeng Liu,Yudong Han,Zhuofan Shi,Sixiong Xie,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SGR-Bench benchmark评估大语言模型在状态门控检索方面的能力——即在专业数据网站通过配置筛选条件、视图、层级等网站特定状态来获取答案。最佳系统仅达66.18% F1，主要失败原因是检索范围漂移(37.2%)和标准不匹配(27.6%)，而非答案组合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing benchmarks focus on general web tasks but overlook specialized retrieval requiring site-specific state configuration (filters, hierarchies, scopes). Answer-bearing evidence only becomes accessible after correctly establishing this retrieval state.

Method: Introduced SGR-Bench with 100 expert-curated tasks across 6 source families and 12 public data ecosystems. Each task has both constraint-guided and goal-oriented formulations for controlled comparison. Evaluated 11 systems (8 CLI agents + 3 commercial products) and manually audited 156 failed trajectories.

Result: Best system achieves 66.18% item-level F1 with much lower row-level F1. Audit of 156 failures reveals: retrieval-scope drift (37.2%), criterion mismatch (27.6%), and answer composition (10.3%). Agents often reach correct sources but configure wrong states.

Conclusion: SGR presents a significant yet underaddressed challenge for current LLM agents; the best systems achieve only ~66% F1, with most failures occurring at the state-establishment stage rather than answer composition. This benchmark provides a controlled way to study explicit vs. implicit guidance for state-gated retrieval.

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and tool-using agents have expanded the range of benchmarked web tasks. Yet an important class of specialized retrieval tasks remains undercharacterized. On many specialized data-retrieval websites, answer-bearing evidence becomes accessible only after establishing the correct site-specific retrieval state through filters, views, hierarchies, or scopes. We term this capability state-gated retrieval (SGR). We introduce SGR-Bench, a benchmark for this setting containing 100 expert-curated tasks spanning six source families and 12 public data ecosystems. Each task requires discovering the appropriate website and configuring its site-specific retrieval state to produce a structured answer. SGR-Bench pairs constraint-guided and goal-oriented formulations of the same underlying problems, enabling controlled comparisons between explicit and implicit guidance for state-gated retrieval. We evaluate eight CLI-based agentic LLM systems and three commercial search-agent products. On SGR-Bench, the strongest system reaches only 66.18% item-level F1, while row-level F1 remains much lower. A manual audit of 156 analyzable failed CLI trajectories shows why: agents often reach a relevant web source, but establish the wrong site-specific retrieval state. Retrieval-scope drift (37.2%) and criterion mismatch (27.6%) dominate, whereas final answer composition accounts for only 10.3%. The dataset and single-case evaluation instructions are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/PKUAIWeb/SGR-BENCH.

</details>


### [118] [Evaluating Large Language Models as Live Strategic Agents: Provider Performance, Hybrid Decomposition, and Operational Gaps in Timed Risk Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22238)
*H. C. Ekne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过实时多阶段游戏环境评估LLM，发现提供商间的性能差异主要来自端到端系统行为而非单纯规划能力，Gemini的优势在于更强的目标追踪和执行转化能力，建议从组件化工作流角度评估LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准测试仅捕捉LLM实际行为的部分特征；真实系统中模型处于有时间限制、格式约束和失败模式的重复循环中。

Method: 使用带时间限制的多阶段《Risk》游戏环境进行评估；设计32场跨提供商锦标赛分离规划与执行；使用统一的Gemini Flash脚手架标准化执行阶段；对保存的规划和执行轨迹进行机制分析。

Result: Gemini-3.1-pro-preview在32场比赛中赢得20场（击败GPT-5.1、Claude-opus-4-7和Kimi-K2.6），差异显著（p≈1.5×10⁻⁵）；但标准化执行后，32场规划者比赛结果接近均等（p≈0.821）；机制分析显示Gemini更频繁引用终端目标并转化更多回合为深度征服链。

Conclusion: 实时智能体表现取决于目标追踪、执行转化、成本和运行时可靠性；建议将LLM作为有界工作流中的组件进行评估，而非孤立的基准测试响应者。

Abstract: Static benchmarks capture only part of how large language models behave in practice. Real systems place models inside repeated loops with time limits, formatting constraints, and failure modes. We study this setting in a timed multi-phase Risk environment with explicit victory targets and repeated planning and execution cycles. In a replicated 32-game cross-provider championship under frozen rules, gemini-3.1-pro-preview won 20 of 32 games against gpt-5.1, claude-opus-4-7, and kimi-k2.6, and the pooled winner distribution differs strongly from an equal-strength null (p approx 1.5 x 10^-5). We then separate planning from execution by standardizing execution on a cheaper Gemini Flash scaffold. Under this design, a pooled 32-game planner bakeoff is consistent with near-equality (p approx 0.821), which indicates that much of the earlier provider spread came from end-to-end system behavior rather than planning alone. To study mechanism, we analyze saved planning and execution traces from the provider championship. Gemini refers to the terminal objective far more often than the other models and increases that focus as victory approaches. Gemini also converts more turns into deep conquest chains, even though it is not the cleanest runtime. These results show that live-agent performance depends on objective tracking, execution conversion, cost, and runtime reliability, and they support evaluating LLMs as components in bounded workflows rather than as isolated benchmark respondents.

</details>


### [119] [Unlocking Proactivity in Task-Oriented Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22240)
*Hongbin Zhang,Ning Gao,Yuqin Dai,Ruiyuan Wu,Jinpeng Wang,Rena Wei Gao,Bingdong Tan,Shuzheng Gao,Zongjie Li,Chaozheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文解决大语言模型在主动任务导向对话（如外呼销售）中的保守性问题。核心发现是用户潜在顾虑是可训练的关键信号。基于此提出认知用户模拟器（Cognitive User Simulator）和非对称视角策略优化训练框架（SIAV-PO），使LLM能够主动探查用户顾虑并引导对话走向接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Post-trained LLMs are inherently conservative and struggle with proactive task-oriented dialogue tasks (e.g., outbound sales). Existing reward-shaping RL methods like GRPO only re-weight what a passive policy samples, failing to unlock proactive capabilities.

Method: Cognitive User Simulator that models users with observable external traits and hidden internal concerns, emitting per-turn state dynamics. Training via Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization (SIAV-PO) comprising Asymmetric On-Policy Self-Distillation and State-Transition Policy Refinement.

Result: Conditioning on user's latent concerns unlocks proactive capability that no amount of sampling can undermine, establishing these concerns as a pivotal training-time signal.

Conclusion: The proposed Cognitive User Simulator combined with SIAV-PO training framework successfully enables proactive persuasion capabilities in LLMs beyond what sampling-based methods can achieve.

Abstract: Proactive task-oriented dialogue (TOD), such as outbound sales, demands a persuasive agent that actively probes the user's concerns and steers the conversation toward acceptance within a bounded number of turns. Yet post-trained LLMs are inherently conservative, and reward-shaping RL (e.g., GRPO) struggles since it only re-weights what an already passive policy samples. We show that conditioning on the user's latent concerns unlocks proactive capability that no amount of sampling can undermine, establishing these concerns as a pivotal training-time signal. To operationalize this finding, we build the \textbf{Cognitive User Simulator}, which models each user as a stratified persona comprising observable external traits and hidden internal concerns. The simulator produces faithful and diverse interactions, while emitting per-turn state dynamics that track persuasion progress. We then introduce \textbf{Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization}, which converts the modeled concerns and the simulation state transition into complementary training objectives: (1) \emph{Asymmetric On-Policy Self-Distillation} that transfers concern-aware behavior from a privileged view of the same policy into its deployable, conversation-only view; and (2) \emph{State-Transition Policy Refinement} ...

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [DynaFlow: Transparent and Flexible Intra-Device Parallelism via Programmable Operator Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21603)
*Yi Pan,Yile Gu,Jinbin Luo,Yibo Wu,Ziren Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shengkai Lin,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DynaFlow通过解耦逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度，实现intra-device并行策略的透明灵活集成，无需侵入式代码改造即可提升ML系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架的静态顺序编程模型与intra-device并行化策略存在根本性冲突，导致集成需侵入式、模型特定的代码改造；同时策略对执行上下文高度敏感，迫使开发者维护多套专用方案。

Method: 提出DynaFlow框架，前端支持图划分注解和自定义intra-device并行策略的可编程接口，后端采用异步控制/数据流管理、定制内存管理消除拷贝开销，同时兼容CUDA Graphs和TorchInductor等优化。

Result: 成功将代表性并行策略集成至6个SOTA ML系统中，代码改动极小，吞吐量提升最高达1.29倍。

Conclusion: DynaFlow框架已公开开源(https://github.com/uw-syfi/DynaFlow)，通过解耦逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度，实现了intra-device并行策略在ML系统中的透明、灵活集成，兼具实用性与工程可行性。

Abstract: Intra-device parallelism addresses resource under-utilization in ML inference and training by overlapping the execution of operators with different resource usage. However, its wide adoption is hindered by a fundamental conflict with the static, sequential programming model of existing frameworks. Integrating these strategies requires invasive, model-specific code overhauls, representing an intractable engineering cost. This is further amplified by the high sensitivity of strategies to execution contexts (e.g., workload, model architecture, hardware), forcing developers to implement and maintain multiple specialized solutions. To address this, we propose DynaFlow, a framework that enables the transparent and flexible integration of intra-device parallelism by decoupling the logical model definition from the physical execution schedule. DynaFlow introduces a flexible frontend with annotations for graph partitioning and a programmable interface for defining custom intra-device parallelism strategies. Its efficient backend manages complex control/data-flow asynchronously, uses custom memory management to eliminate copy overheads, and preserves compatibility with optimizations like CUDA Graphs and TorchInductor. We demonstrate that DynaFlow can integrate representative parallelism strategies into 6 state-of-the-art ML systems with minimal code changes, achieving up to a 1.29x throughput improvement. DynaFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/uw-syfi/DynaFlow.

</details>


### [121] [LiveR: Fine-Grained Elasticity via Live Reconfiguration for Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22014)
*Haoyuan Liu,Kairui Zhou,Shuyao Qi,Qinwei Yang,Shengkai Lin,Shizhen Zhao,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LiveR是一个用于弹性LLM训练的实时重配置运行时，通过在混合并行训练世界之间进行有界内存的实时交接，替代传统的基于存储的检查点/重启方式。该系统在当前世界继续训练的同时异步准备目标世界，通过高带宽互联直接流式传输模型状态，在线重塑张量/流水线/数据并行维度，实现秒级配置切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型模型训练越来越多地利用不稳定但廉价的GPU资源（如spot实例），但现有弹性训练系统将重配置视为停止-重启过程：通过检查点外部化分布式状态、在新拓扑上重建运行时并重启训练，每次调整大小都成为存储密集型的恢复过程，产生大量停机时间（检查点I/O、进程重启、CUDA初始化、通信器设置等）。

Method: LiveR采用异步准备+实时交接的方案：(1)在当前世界继续训练的同时异步准备目标世界；(2)在隔离环境中引导新加入的worker，使重量级初始化脱离关键路径；(3)通过高带宽互联直接流式传输模型状态；(4)在线重塑张量、流水线和数据并行维度的模型状态；(5)执行轻量级提交，切换到新配置而无需停止-重启。

Result: 在多节点GPU集群的多种重配置场景中：(1)将停机时间从分钟级降至秒级；(2)相比检查点/重启基线加速14×-23×；(3)稳态开销极小；(4)在资源波动条件下可维持99%的训练吞吐效率。

Conclusion: LiveR使不稳定低成本GPU资源在LLM训练中更加实用，通过消除停止-重启瓶颈，显著降低了弹性训练的开销和停机时间，为大规模分布式训练提供了更灵活的资源利用方案。

Abstract: To reduce user costs and maximize cluster utilization, large model training increasingly leverages volatile but inexpensive GPU capacity, such as spot instances and reclaimable resources in shared clusters. Yet, capitalizing on these economic benefits requires jobs to adapt within the short warning windows that many such environments provide. Existing elastic training systems still treat reconfiguration as stop-and-restart: they externalize distributed state through checkpoints, rebuild the distributed runtime on a new topology, and restart training, turning each resize event into a storage-heavy recovery procedure that incurs substantial downtime from checkpoint I/O, process restart, CUDA initialization, and communicator setup. We present LiveR, a live reconfiguration runtime for elastic LLM training that replaces storage-backed restart with a live, bounded-memory handoff between mixed-parallel training worlds. While the current world continues training, LiveR asynchronously prepares the target world, bootstraps newly added workers in isolation to keep heavyweight initialization off the critical path, and streams model state directly over high-bandwidth interconnects while reshaping it online across tensor, pipeline, and data parallel dimensions. Once the target world is ready, LiveR performs a lightweight commit that switches training to the new configuration without stop-and-restart on the live path. We implement LiveR atop Megatron-LM and PyTorch and evaluate it end-to-end on a multi-node GPU cluster. Across diverse reconfiguration scenarios, LiveR reduces downtime from minutes to seconds, accelerates reconfiguration by 14$\times$-23$\times$ over checkpoint/restart baselines, incurs minimal steady-state overhead, and sustains up to 99% training goodput under volatile-resource conditions, making volatile low-cost GPU capacity far more practical for LLM training.

</details>


### [122] [Secure and Parallel Determinant Computation for Large-Scale Matrices in Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22039)
*Prajwal Panth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SPDC框架，在N个分布式边缘服务器上实现隐私保护的矩阵行列式计算(MDC)。通过复合元素失真(CED)方法（包括元素级混淆EWO和Panth旋转定理PRT）结合并行LU分解，在单向通信模型下实现安全、高效、可扩展的行列式计算，并提供两种轻量级结果验证算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行列式算法具有立方复杂度，在资源受限的边缘物联网环境中难以实现实时处理；同时，敏感矩阵数据在分布式边缘服务器上存在隐私泄露风险，需要在保证计算效率的同时实现隐私保护。

Method: 1) 使用复合元素失真(CED)方法进行轻量级加密，结合元素级混淆(EWO)和Panth旋转定理(PRT)保护矩阵结构和数值内容；2) 采用并行LU分解将加密矩阵块分布到任意数量的不可信边缘服务器；3) 单向通信模型消除服务器间交互；4) 两种验证算法Q_2（概率标量方法）和Q_3（确定性低复杂度方法）确保结果完整性。

Result: 框架在数学分析上证明能够提供强隐私安全保障、低计算开销和高部署灵活性，适用于分布式边缘辅助系统中的安全、可扩展、实时矩阵行列式计算。

Conclusion: SPDC框架通过轻量级加密与并行计算相结合，有效解决了边缘计算环境中资源受限与隐私安全之间的矛盾，为控制、密码学、机器学习等应用提供可行的实时计算方案。

Abstract: The advent of edge computing has enabled resource-constrained clients to delegate intensive computational tasks to distributed edge servers, especially within Internet of Things (IoT) environments. Among such tasks, Matrix Determinant Computation (MDC) remains critical for applications in control systems, cryptography, and machine learning. However, the cubic complexity of traditional determinant algorithms makes them unsuitable for real-time processing in constrained edge scenarios.
  We propose a Secure Parallel Determinant Computation (SPDC) framework, which provides strong security guaranties, including privacy-preserving MDC, across N distributed edge servers. The framework achieves privacy through Composite Element Distortion (CED) - a lightweight encryption method that combines Element-wise Obfuscation (EWO) and the Panth Rotation Theorem (PRT) to conceal both structural and numerical matrix content while preserving determinant properties. Parallel LU decomposition is used to distribute encrypted matrix blocks across an arbitrary number of untrusted edge servers, enabling efficient and scalable determinant computation. A one-way communication model further reduces coordination overhead by eliminating inter-server interactions. To ensure result integrity with minimal client burden, we further introduce two verification algorithms: Q_2, a probabilistic scalar method, and Q_3, a deterministic and low-complexity alternative.
  Mathematical analysis demonstrates that the proposed framework provides strong privacy and security guaranties, low computational overhead, and deployment flexibility - making it well-suited for secure, scalable, and real-time MDC in distributed edge-assisted systems.

</details>


### [123] [Nf-PEAK: Process-Based Energy Attribution for Nextflow Workflows on Kubernetes Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22393)
*Philipp Thamm,Somayeh Mohammadi,Kathleen West,Knut Reinert,Lauritz Thamsen,Ulf Leser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Nf-PEAK方法，通过容器化方式将CPU和DRAM能耗归因到Kubernetes集群上运行的单个进程和Nextflow任务。该方法结合cgroup元数据映射、RA PL采样和非线性能耗模型，在nf-core工作流测试中达到6.6%~10.9%的平均绝对百分比误差，优于现有Kepler工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流在共享Kubernetes集群上的能耗对成本和可持续性至关重要。任务级能耗估计面临挑战：Intel RAPL仅提供节点级数据、计数器访问受限、并发工作负载引入测量噪声。

Method: Nf-PEAK包含四个步骤：(i)识别工作流Pod；(ii)通过cgroup元数据将Pod映射到宿主机进程；(iii)采样RAPL和进程级性能计数器；(iv)应用非线性能耗 credited模型后聚合到任务级别。

Result: 在Kubernetes集群上评估三种nf-core工作流，Nf-PEAK在独立运行时平均MAPE为6.6%，在8/32线程被占用时为10.9%，在2/3/4/8节点上均保持稳定。相比state-of-the-art的Kepler工具，Nf-PEAK平均误差更低，尤其在共置负载下表现更优。

Conclusion: Nf-PEAK能有效实现细粒度的任务级能耗归因，为科学工作流的能耗优化提供了可靠的测量基础。

Abstract: Scientific workflows are pipelines of interdependent tasks. They are increasingly executed on shared Kubernetes clusters via workflow engines such as Nextflow. Their energy consumption matters for both cost and sustainability. It is necessary to examine and optimize workflow tasks individually, because they can be very heterogeneous. However, estimating task-level energy on clusters is difficult: Intel RAPL counters report only node-level energy, access to counters and host process information is typically restricted, and concurrent workloads introduce resource contention and measurement noise. We present Nf-PEAK, a containerized method to attribute CPU-package and DRAM energy to individual processes and Nextflow tasks. Nf-PEAK (i) identifies workflow pods, (ii) maps pods to host processes via cgroup metadata, (iii) samples RAPL and per-process performance counters, and (iv) applies a non-linear energy-credit model before aggregating results at task level. On a Kubernetes cluster, we evaluate three nf-core workflows under controlled co-located CPU load. Nf-PEAK reaches an average Mean Absolute Percentage Error of 6.6% in isolated runs and 10.9% when an unrelated workload saturates 8 of 32 hardware threads per node, and remains stable across 2, 3, 4, and 8 nodes. Compared to the state-of-the-art Kubernetes tool Kepler, Nf-PEAK yields lower error on average, particularly under co-located load.

</details>


### [124] [AI-Driven Multi-Region Provisioning for Cloud Services Using Spot Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22778)
*Javier Fabra,Enrique Molina-Giménez,Pedro García-López*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: This paper presents an AI-driven multi-region spot fleet provisioning service that predicts costs and configurations before launch, achieving high prediction accuracy (99.79%) and significant cost savings (up to 64%) compared to AWS EC2 Spot Service's single-region limitation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cloud platforms need elastic infrastructures to handle dynamic workloads. Spot instances offer discounted resources but are risky due to uncertain pricing, availability, and interruption risks across regions. Existing tools like AWS EC2 Spot Service lack multi-region support and cannot estimate costs before deployment.

Method: The approach combines real-time monitoring of provisioning plans with predictive models to estimate fleet configurations and prices before launch. The system preserves the operational behavior of the EC2 Spot Service while enabling cross-region deployment decisions.

Result: The system demonstrates 99.79% prediction accuracy against the actual EC2 Spot Service and achieves potential cost savings of up to 64% through multi-region price variability exploitation.

Conclusion: A cost-aware, multi-region spot fleet provisioning system was developed and validated with fleets of up to 1500 vCPUs, achieving 99.79% price prediction accuracy and potential cost savings of up to 64% by leveraging regional price variability.

Abstract: Cloud service platforms increasingly rely on elastic infrastructures to support dynamic workloads. Spot instances provide discounted computing resources but introduce uncertainty due to dynamic pricing, resource availability, and interruption risks that vary across geographical regions. In Amazon Web Services, the EC2 Spot Service simplifies fleet provisioning through allocation strategies, but it cannot estimate fleet costs before deployment and restricts provisioning to a single region. This paper presents an AI-driven provisioning service for multi-region spot fleets. The proposed approach combines monitoring of provisioning plans with predictive models to estimate fleet configurations and prices before launch, enabling cost-aware deployment decisions across regions while preserving the operational behavior of the EC2 Spot Service. The system was validated with fleets of up to 1500 vCPUs. Experimental results show a prediction accuracy of 99.79% compared to the EC2 Spot Service and potential cost savings of up to 64% by exploiting regional price variability.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Argo: Efficient Importance Labeling for Enterprise Email Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21604)
*Siddhant Ray,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Kevin Chian,Yan Guo,Cristina St Hill,Jack W. Stokes,Victor Wang,Junchen Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Argo框架，通过智能搜索成本-质量权衡空间和按需供给机制，使企业能够在保持接近GPT标注质量的同时，大幅降低推理和profiling成本，实现大规模上下文感知邮件标注的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业邮件重要性标注是关键需求，传统方法（关键词匹配、用户规则、发送者启发式）依赖大量人工特征工程，且扩展性和泛化能力不足；而GPT-4.1等大规模LLM虽然标注质量优异，但企业级邮件量下的计算成本过高，难以实际部署。

Method: 构建了Argo企业邮件标注框架，包含：(1)profiler模块用于高效搜索标注方案的成本-质量权衡空间，识别高性价比的替代方案；(2)按需供给机制，根据实时负载智能扩展，以最小化峰值推理成本增量。

Result: 在3个开源邮件数据集上，Argo实现了148-167倍的推理成本降低（质量损失可忽略），同时 profiling成本降低20-640000倍。

Conclusion: 通过创新的成本-质量权衡搜索和智能负载调度机制，Argo成功实现了企业级邮件重要性标注的大规模实际部署，解决了LLM应用中的成本瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Email importance labeling has long been a critical yet challenging problem for businesses and individuals. Traditional approaches; such as keyword matching, user-defined rules, and sender-based heuristics; demand extensive manual feature engineering and fail to scale effectively or generalize. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong potential and a natural fit for this task, offering deep contextual understanding and superior labeling quality. However, using LLM models like GPT-4.1 at enterprise email volumes incurs prohibitive computational costs and hinders real-world deployment. We explore the trade-off space of using alternative labeling schemes as opposed to GPT4.1 scale LLMs, with the goal of achieving near GPT level labeling quality with significantly lower cost. We develop Argo, an enterprise email labeling framework, where we construct a profiler to efficiently search the cost quality trade-off space of labeling and identify cost-efficient alternatives to labeling emails. Additionally, we design an on-demand provisioning scheme to intelligently scale Argo with real time load, to minimize cost increases during peak load inference. Over 3 open-source email datasets, Argo achieves 148-167X inference cost reduction with negligible quality degradation and 20-640000X lower profiling costs, making large-scale, context-aware email labeling practical for enterprises.

</details>


### [126] [Emergence of agriculture in an artificial society of reinforcement learning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22256)
*Gautier Hamon,Martí Sánchez-Fibla,Clément Moulin-Frier,Ricard Solé*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究通过人工强化学习智能体社会模拟，发现农业实践可在无显式指令下自发涌现，其转变受四个关键要素驱动：延迟奖励评估、反作弊机制、社会学习稳定化及不可逆锁定效应，揭示了个体决策、社会互动与生态反馈之间的通用耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业起源是一个重大的进化转变，迫切需要理解从简单互动如何涌现出复杂的集体行为这一基本科学问题。

Method: 构建了嵌入动态生态环境的人工强化学习智能体社会，模拟农业实践的自发涌现，无需显式指令，通过学习与环境 Modification的耦合动力学产生农业行为。

Result: 识别出农业转变的四个关键要素：个体规划（延迟奖励评估）、社会脆弱性（作弊者入侵）、社会学习稳定化（作为"防火墙"抑制作弊者）、锁定效应（农业一旦建立便不可逆）；社会学习能实现持续人口增长和驯化资源的非线性放大。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了农业起源背后的通用机制：个体决策（延迟奖励评估）、社会互动（反作弊机制）、生态反馈（资源驯化）的耦合动力学，并强调人工社会作为研究文化创新和重大进化转变的实验平台潜力。

Abstract: The origin of agriculture represents a major evolutionary transition and a paradigmatic example of how complex collective behaviors emerge from simple interactions. Here we introduce an artificial society of reinforcement learning agents embedded in a dynamic ecological environment to identify general principles underlying this transition. Within this system, agricultural practices emerge spontaneously - without explicit instruction - through the coupled dynamics of learning and environmental modification. We show that this transition is governed by four key ingredients: individual planning through the valuation of delayed rewards, social vulnerability to cheaters, stabilization via social learning, and an emergent lock-in effect that renders agriculture effectively irreversible once established. In particular, we demonstrate that social learning acts as a "firewall" that suppresses cheater invasion and enables the propagation of successful strategies, leading to sustained population growth and nonlinear amplification of domesticated resources. Together, these results reveal universal mechanisms linking individual decision-making, social interactions, and ecological feedbacks. More broadly, they highlight the potential of artificial societies as experimental platforms to study the emergence of cultural innovations and major evolutionary transitions.

</details>


### [127] [Sibyl-AutoResearch: Autonomous Research Needs Self-Evolving Trial-and-Error Harnesses, Not Paper Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22343)
*Chengcheng Wang,Qinhua Xie,Wei He,Jianyuan Guo,Shiqi Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对当前自主研究系统缺乏试验经验和研究判断能力的问题，提出了Sibyl-AutoResearch框架，通过Scientific Trial-and-Error Harnesses实现试验经验的系统化捕捉和利用，核心是两个转换单元（trial-to-behavior和trial-to-harness-behavior），并在SIBYL系统中验证了框架的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主研究系统虽能执行科学工作流（提出想法、运行代码、检查结果、起草论文），但缺乏研究判断能力和试验经验。具体问题包括：弱证据被直接写成文本、试点信号被过度泛化为宽泛声明、记忆仅停留在文本层面、重复性过程失败不会改变后续行为。这些系统无法从试验经验中学习和改进。

Method: 提出了Scientific Trial-and-Error Harnesses（科学试错框架），使智能体能运行有界试验、保存正负结果，并将经验路由到后续规划、验证、声明范围调整、排程、批评、写作和框架修复。核心贡献是两个可审计的转换单元：1）trial-to-behavior conversion（试验-行为转换）：将试验信号链接到后续研究行动；2）trial-to-harness-behavior conversion（试验-框架行为转换）：将重复性过程失败链接到系统更新。系统实现为文件支持的SIBYL自主研究系统，暴露状态、角色、记忆、门控和工件追踪。

Result: 通过回顾性审计识别出8个高置信度转换事件（中位延迟1轮，最大延迟3轮）；恢复性失败注册表显示5类自然发生的失败被阻止、降级或路由到后续修复。

Conclusion: 该框架通过SIBYL系统实现，审计追踪了8个高置信度转换事件（中位延迟1轮，最多3轮），并记录了5类自然发生的失败（重复结果、过时数字、不支持的统计等）的处理情况。研究表明所提出的转换单元可从实际自主研究工作空间中恢复，但不构成比较性能声明。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems increasingly make the scientific workflow executable: agents can propose ideas, run code, inspect results, and draft papers. But executable workflows do not by themselves produce research judgment. We analyze where current systems lose trial experience: weak evidence becomes prose, pilot signals become broad claims, memory remains textual, and recurring process failures do not change later behavior. We introduce Sibyl-AutoResearch, a self-evolving AutoResearch framework built around Scientific Trial-and-Error Harnesses. A harness lets agents run bounded trials, preserve positive and negative outcomes, and route lessons into later planning, validation, claim scope, scheduling, critique, writing, and harness repair. We formalize this through two auditable conversion units: trial-to-behavior conversion, which links trial signals to later research actions, and trial-to-harness-behavior conversion, which links recurring process failures to system updates. We implement the framework in SIBYL, a file-backed autonomous research system that exposes the state, roles, memory, gates, and artifact traces needed to inspect these conversion paths. A retrospective audit identifies eight high-confidence conversion events, with a median latency of one iteration and a maximum latency of three iterations. A recovered-failure registry further shows how five naturally occurring failure classes, including duplicate results, stale numbers, and unsupported statistics, were blocked, downgraded, or routed into later repair. These traces do not establish a comparative performance claim; they show that the proposed conversion units are recoverable from realistic autonomous-research workspaces. The SIBYL framework and system are available at https://github.com/Sibyl-Research-Team/AutoResearch-SibylSystem.

</details>


### [128] [A Generalized Nash Equilibrium-Seeking Scheme for Trauma Resuscitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22661)
*Promise Ekpo,Angelique Taylor,Lekan Molu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Abstract: Trauma resuscitation is a clinical process for treating life-threatening physiological disorders in safety-critical environments, driven by the experience of healthcare workers (HCWs). Designing and optimizing quantifiable metrics that accurately capture HCW decisions may augment current resuscitation procedures with the potential to improve patient outcomes. This motivates our socio-technical formulation of trauma resuscitation as a distributed generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE)-seeking game with coupled inequality constraints. This method is optimized over a time-varying communication graph. We introduce novel insights from clinical experience to model HCWs behavior. This work facilitates the best possible resuscitation outcome given HCWs workloads, schedules, competencies, and limited resources.

</details>


### [129] [Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems via Decentralized Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22721)
*Guangya Hao,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Self-evolving multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising route to LLM agents that continually improve from experience, with persistent memory at their foundation. However, existing designs almost exclusively adopt a centralized repository shared across agents, incurring communication and coordination overhead, raising privacy concerns, and collapsing agent diversity. We propose DecentMem, a decentralized memory framework in which each agent maintains its own dual-pool memory -- an exploitation pool of consolidated past trajectories and an exploration pool of LLM-generated candidates for unseen contexts. The two pools are reweighted online based on stage-wise feedback from an LLM-as-a-judge. Theoretically, we prove that this design guarantees global reachability of the solution space and achieves $O(\log T)$ cumulative regret, matching the stochastic bandit lower bound up to constants. In practice, across three MAS frameworks (AutoGen, DyLAN, AgentNet), three Qwen3 backbones (4B/8B/14B), two Gemma4 backbones (E2B/E4B) and five benchmarks spanning math, code, QA, and embodied tasks, DecentMem improves average accuracy by up to 23.8% over the strongest centralized memory baseline and by up to 52.5% over the no-memory baseline, while reducing token usage by up to 49%.

</details>

### [130] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TCT框架，通过自监督对比学习捕获金融交易序列中的时序行为模式。实验表明纯嵌入可达到AUC 0.8644，但与领域特征结合后未能超越强基线模型（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），提示习得表示与人工特征存在重叠。该工作处于时序表示学习发展的中间阶段，为未来在模型架构、训练目标和集成策略方面的研究提供了方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融交易序列中蕴含着重要的上下文时序动态信息，需要有效的表示学习方法来捕获这些行为模式，以支持下游欺诈检测任务。

Method: 采用自监督对比学习目标训练Temporal Contrastive Transformer模型，将习得的嵌入向量作为输入特征，配合梯度提升分类器进行下游欺诈检测任务评估。

Result: 纯嵌入特征可达到有意义的预测性能（AUC 0.8644），表明模型成功捕获了非平凡的时序结构。但与领域工程特征结合后，相比基线未显示出可测量的提升（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），说明习得表示与现有特征抽象存在较大重叠。

Conclusion: TCT是一个有前景的表示学习方法，能够捕获相关行为信号，但目前还无法在强领域特征工程基线上取得增量提升。模型性能与精心设计的特征工程基线相当（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），说明习得的表示近似于领域特定特征，无需手工工程即可达到相似水平。尚未达到生产就绪状态，但为减少金融犯罪检测中对特征工程的依赖指明了有希望的方向。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [131] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探索了大语言模型能否在实验前预测研究想法的实证成功。通过监督微调，8B参数模型达到77.1%准确率，超越GPT-5；强化学习（RLVR）方法实现71.35%并提供可解释推理路径。证明小型高效模型可作为有效的科研想法验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. There's a need to forecast empirical success before experiments are run.

Method: Comparative empirical forecasting with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). Models predict which of two research ideas will achieve better benchmark performance on PapersWithCode data (11,488 idea pairs).

Result: SFT dramatically boosts performance from 30% to 77.1% (outperforming GPT-5 at 61.1%). RLVR achieves 71.35% with interpretable reasoning paths. Models show robustness to surface heuristics and transferability across domains and time.

Conclusion: Small language models (8B parameters) can serve as effective, objective verifiers for evaluating AI-generated research ideas, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery. The approach demonstrates robustness across domains and time periods.

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [132] [The Attribution Impossibility: No Feature Ranking Is Faithful, Stable, and Complete Under Collinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21492)
*Drake Caraker,Bryan Arnold,David Rhoads*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究证明了特征共线性下不可能同时实现忠实、稳定、完备的排序，通过DASH集成方法解决，并在Lean 4中形式化验证了305条定理。归因设计空间仅存在两类方法：不稳定但忠实完备的方法，以及稳定但需要绑定对称特征的集成方法如DASH。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征共线性是机器学习中的普遍现象，但现有特征重要性排序方法在共线性存在时表现不稳定，存在排名翻转问题，影响可解释性和公平性审计的可靠性。

Method: 1. 证明特征共线性下不可能同时满足忠实性、稳定性和完备性的数学定理；2. 对四类模型（梯度提升、Lasso、随机森林、线性模型）进行量化分析；3. 提出解决方案DASH（Diversified Aggregation of SHAP）；4. 在Lean 4中形式化验证（305条定理，16条公理，0 sorry）。

Result: 1. 完整刻画归因设计空间：仅存在两类方法——忠实-完备方法（不稳定，排名翻转达50%）与DASH等集成方法（稳定，对对称特征报告绑定）；2. 量化不可能性：梯度提升中归因比发散为1/(1-ρ²)，Lasso中为无穷，随机森林中收敛；3. DASH具有紧集大小公式，达到Cramer-Rao方差界；4. 77个公开数据集实验：68%存在归因不稳定性；5. 条件SHAP在等因果效应下仍无法避免不可能性；6. SHAP-based公平性审计在共线性下不可靠。

Conclusion: 提出了两个互补的方法族：忠实-完备方法（不稳定）与集成方法如DASH（稳定）。DASH在无偏聚合中达到Pareto最优，实现Cramer-Rao方差界。形式化验证了可解释AI中的首个不可能性定理，对公平性审计具有直接意义。
